```
102	   *  重复 id 折叠成位图语义——原版 nearbyNPCsByType 布尔） */
103	  nearby: number[];

107	  rand?: () => number;
108	}

110	export interface HappinessResult {
111	  /** 价格乘子（1.0 = 无修正；1000 = 心情崩坏） */
112	  priceMul: number;
113	  /** 心情报告 key（原版 HappinessReport——中文提示用） */
114	  report: string[];
115	}
116	
117	/** GetShoppingSettings / ProcessMood 1:1 */
118	export function computeHappiness(inp: HappinessInput): HappinessResult {
119	  let mul = 1.0;
120	  const report: string[] = [];
121	  // remix/旅行商(368)/骷髅商(453)/城镇宠物/IsNotReallyTownNPC(老人37)——直接 1.0
122	  if (inp.npcVanillaId === 368 || inp.npcVanillaId === 453 || inp.npcVanillaId === 37) {
123	    return { priceMul: 1, report: [] };
124	  }
125	  // 无家 / 离家 >120 / 危险群系 → 1000（:111-122）
126	  if (inp.homeless) report.push('TownNPCMoodNo.Home');
127	  else if (inp.distFromHome > 120) report.push('TownNPCMoodNo.FarFromHome');
128	  if (inp.homeless || inp.distFromHome > 120
129	    || inp.playerInCorrupt || inp.playerInCrimson || inp.playerInDungeon) {
130	    return { priceMul: 1000, report };
131	  }
132	  // 拥挤/宽敞（:126-157）
133	  const princess = inp.isPrincess;
134	  if (princess) {
135	    if (inp.npcsInHouse < 2 && inp.npcsInVillage < 2) {
136	      report.push('TownNPCMoodPrincess.HateLonely');
137	      return { priceMul: 1000, report };
138	    }
139	  } else if (inp.npcsInHouse > 3) {
140	    for (let i = 3; i < inp.npcsInHouse; i++) mul *= 1.05;
141	    report.push(inp.npcsInHouse > 6 ? 'TownNPCMoodShop.HateCrowded' : 'TownNPCMoodShop.DislikeCrowded');
142	  }
143	  if (!princess && inp.npcsInHouse <= 2 && inp.npcsInVillage < 4) {
144	    report.push('TownNPCMoodShop.LoveSpace');
145	    mul *= 0.95;
146	  }
147	  // 生物群系偏好（BiomePreferenceListTrait.ModifyShopPrice）
148	  const pref = BIOME_PREFS[inp.npcVanillaId];
149	  if (pref) {
150	    if (pref.like && inp.zones[pref.like]) {
151	      mul *= inp.npcVanillaId === 142 ? LOVE_MUL : LIKE_MUL;
152	      report.push(`TownNPCMoodShopper.${inp.npcVanillaId === 142 ? 'LoveBiome' : 'LikeBiome'}`);
153	    }
154	    if (pref.dislike && inp.zones[pref.dislike]) {
155	      mul *= inp.npcVanillaId === 142 ? HATE_MUL : DISLIKE_MUL;
156	      report.push(`TownNPCMoodShopper.${inp.npcVanillaId === 142 ? 'HateBiome' : 'DislikeBiome'}`);
157	    }
158	  }
159	  // NPC 互相关系（AllPersonalitiesModifier.ModifyShopPrice_Relationships :12-506——全部命中连乘）
160	  const near = new Set(inp.nearby);
161	  if (princess) {
162	    // 公主无静态表：同屋池随机抽 3 个各 Love ×0.88（不足 3 全 Love；:15-36）
163	    const pool = [...near];
164	    const rand = inp.rand ?? Math.random;
165	    for (let j = 0; j < 3 && pool.length > 0; j++) {
166	      const idx = Math.floor(rand() * pool.length) % pool.length;
167	      pool.splice(idx, 1);
168	      mul *= LOVE_MUL;
169	      report.push('TownNPCMoodShop.LoveNpc');
170	    }
171	  } else {
172	    if (near.has(663)) { mul *= LIKE_MUL; report.push('TownNPCMoodShop.LikePrincess'); } // :37-40
173	    for (const rel of NPC_RELATIONSHIPS[inp.npcVanillaId] ?? []) {
174	      if (near.has(rel.target)) {
175	        mul *= REL_MUL[rel.level];
176	        report.push(`TownNPCMoodShop.${rel.level}Npc`);
177	      }
178	    }
179	  }
180	  // LimitAndRoundMultiplier（:182-187）
181	  mul = Math.max(0.75, Math.min(1.5, mul));
182	  mul = Math.round(mul * 100) / 100;
183	  if (report.length === 0) report.push('TownNPCMoodShop.Content');
184	  return { priceMul: mul, report };
185	}
```

```
34	  animation: sw-portrait-hop 0.35s cubic-bezier(.2,1.6,.4,1); }
35	@keyframes sw-portrait-hop { 0% { transform: translateY(14px) scale(0.9); opacity: 0.2; }
36	  100% { transform: translateY(0) scale(1); opacity: 1; } }
37	.sw-npc-main { flex: 1; min-width: 0; }
38	.sw-npc-name { color: #ffe8a0; font-size: 15px; margin-bottom: 6px;
39	  text-shadow: 1px 1px 0 #000, -1px -1px 0 #000; }
40	.sw-npc-chat { font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.6; color: #e8e8f4; min-height: 44px; }
41	.sw-npc-btns { display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-top: 8px; }
42	.sw-npc-btns button {
43	  background: #3a4680; color: #e8e8f4; border: 1px solid #7d92d6; border-radius: 4px;
44	  padding: 5px 16px; cursor: pointer; font-family: inherit;
45	}

47	.sw-npc-shop {
48	  position: fixed; left: 50%; top: 50%; transform: translate(-50%, -50%);

66	.sw-shop-item img { width: 26px; height: 26px; image-rendering: pixelated; }
67	.sw-shop-item .nm { flex: 1; }

72	/** 铜币计价格式化(原版 tooltip:金/银/铜) */
73	export function formatCopper(v: number): string {
74	  const gold = Math.floor(v / 10000);
75	  const silver = Math.floor((v % 10000) / 100);
76	  const copper = v % 100;
77	  let s = '';
78	  if (gold) s += `${gold}金`;
79	  if (silver) s += `${silver}银`;
80	  if (copper || !s) s += `${copper}铜`;
81	  return s;
82	}
83	
84	export class NpcDialog {
85	  private panel: HTMLElement | null = null;
86	  private escHandler: ((e: KeyboardEvent) => void) | null = null;
87	
88	  constructor(private parent: HTMLElement, private onButton: (id: NpcButtonId) => void) {
89	    if (!document.getElementById('sw-npc-style')) {
90	      const style = document.createElement('style');
91	      style.id = 'sw-npc-style';
92	      style.textContent = CSS;
93	      document.head.appendChild(style);
94	    }
95	  }
96	
97	  /** 打开对话框:name=NPC 名,chat=台词,buttons=选项(原版 Lang.inter 标签) */
98	  open(name: string, chat: string, buttons: Array<{ id: NpcButtonId; label: string }>, portrait?: string | null) {
99	    this.close();
100	    const panel = document.createElement('div');
101	    panel.className = 'sw-npc-dialog';
102	    const btns = buttons.map((b) => `<button data-b="${b.id}">${b.label}</button>`).join('');
103	    // 头像(原版 Main.npcChatPortrait = Images/TownNPCs/Portraits/Portrait_<Name>，SetTalkNPC :4196;
104	    // 打开时 DoNPCPortraitHop 弹跳)——无头像类型(如骷髅商人外的野兽)原版也不显示
105	    const portraitHtml = portrait
106	      ? `<img class="sw-npc-portrait" src="${portrait}">`
107	      : '';
108	    panel.innerHTML = `<div class="sw-npc-body">${portraitHtml}<div class="sw-npc-main">`
109	      + `<div class="sw-npc-name">${name}</div><div class="sw-npc-chat">${chat}</div>`
110	      + `<div class="sw-npc-btns">${btns}</div></div></div>`;
111	    panel.querySelectorAll('button').forEach((el) => {
112	      el.addEventListener('click', () => {
113	        UISfx.click(); // 原版对话按钮 MenuTick(id 12)
114	        this.onButton((el as HTMLElement).dataset.b as NpcButtonId);
115	      });
116	    });
117	    this.parent.appendChild(panel);
118	    this.panel = panel;
119	    this.escHandler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.code === 'Escape') this.onButton('close'); };
120	    window.addEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
121	  }
122	
123	  close() {
124	    if (this.escHandler) window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
125	    this.escHandler = null;
126	    this.panel?.remove();
127	    this.panel = null;
128	  }
129	
130	  get isOpen(): boolean { return this.panel !== null; }
131	}
132	
133	export class NpcShop {
134	  private panel: HTMLElement | null = null;
135	  private escHandler: ((e: KeyboardEvent) => void) | null = null;
136	
137	  constructor(private parent: HTMLElement, private onBuy: (vanillaId: number) => void, private onClose: () => void) {}
138	
139	  open(title: string, items: ShopEntry[], copper: number) {
140	    this.close();
141	    const panel = document.createElement('div');
142	    panel.className = 'sw-npc-shop';
143	    const rows = items.map((it) => `
144	      <div class="sw-shop-item${copper < it.price ? ' poor' : ''}" data-id="${it.vanillaId}" data-ok="${copper >= it.price ? 1 : 0}">
145	        ${it.iconUrl ? `<img src="${it.iconUrl}">` : '<span style="width:26px;height:26px;display:inline-block"></span>'}
146	        <span class="nm">${it.name}</span><span class="pr">${formatCopper(it.price)}</span>
147	      </div>`).join('');
148	    panel.innerHTML = `
149	      <div class="sw-shop-title">${title}</div>
150	      <div class="sw-shop-coins">${formatCopper(copper)}</div>
151	      <div class="sw-shop-list">${rows}</div>
152	      <div class="sw-shop-foot"><button class="sw-set-back" style="background:#3a4680;color:#e8e8f4;border:1px solid #7d92d6;border-radius:4px;padding:5px 16px;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;">${Lang.inter(52)}</button></div>`;
153	    panel.querySelectorAll('.sw-shop-item').forEach((el) => {
154	      el.addEventListener('click', () => {
155	        if ((el as HTMLElement).dataset.ok !== '1') return;
156	        this.onBuy(Number((el as HTMLElement).dataset.id));
157	      });
158	    });
159	    (panel.querySelector('.sw-set-back') as HTMLElement).addEventListener('click', () => this.onClose());
160	    this.parent.appendChild(panel);
161	    this.panel = panel;
162	    this.escHandler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.code === 'Escape') this.onClose(); };
163	    window.addEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
164	  }
165	
166	  close() {
167	    if (this.escHandler) window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
168	    this.escHandler = null;
169	    this.panel?.remove();
170	    this.panel = null;
171	  }
172	
173	  get isOpen(): boolean { return this.panel !== null; }
174	}
175
```

```
9680	  npcDialogButton(id: NpcButtonId): void {

9693	      if (this.boss) { this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.BossActive')); return; }
9694	      // 原版:释放诅咒时老人消失,击败骷髅王后转为裁缝
9695	      npc.dead = true;
9696	      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.OldManScream'));
9697	      this.closeNpcDialog();
9698	      this.summonBossAtTx('skeletron_head', Math.floor(npc.cx / TILE));
9699	      return;
9700	    }
9701	    if (id === 'quest' && npc.vanillaId === 369) { this.anglerQuestTurnIn(); return; }

9706	  /** 当日任务鱼 id（Main.anglerQuestItemNetIDs[Main.anglerQuest]，Main.cs:20091 同式） */
9707	  anglerQuestItemId(): number { return ANGLER_QUEST_ITEMS[this.world.anglerQuest] ?? 0; }
9708	
9709	  /** Main.AnglerQuestSwap（Main.cs:3799-3947）：清今日交付名单/完成旗 + 按世界门重掷。
9710	   *  调用点 = 黎明（UpdateTime_StartDay :64852）+ 新世界（WorldGen.cs:22655）。
9711	   *  掷骰注入按「种子 ^ 天数」播种的独立 UnifiedRandom → 同种子同天数同任务鱼
9712	   *  （读档/联机一致；原版掷 Main.rand 不可复现，拒绝语义逐条不变） */
9713	  anglerQuestSwap(): void {
9714	    const w = this.world;
9715	    const f = w.flags;
9716	    w.anglerWhoFinishedToday = [];          // :3805
9717	    w.anglerQuestFinished = false;          // :3806
9718	    // :3807 flag = downedBoss1||downedBoss2||downedBoss3||hardMode||downedSlimeKing||downedQueenBee
9719	    const anyBoss = !!(f['downed_4'] || f.downedEyeOfCthulhu          // downedBoss1（EoC）
9720	      || f['downed_13'] || f['downed_266']                            // downedBoss2（世吞/克脑）
9721	      || f.downedSkeletron || f['downed_35']                          // downedBoss3（骷髅王）
9722	      || f.hardMode
9723	      || f.downedSlimeKing || f['downed_50']                          // downedSlimeKing
9724	      || f['downed_222']);                                            // downedQueenBee
9725	    w.anglerQuest = rollAnglerQuest({
9726	      hardMode: !!f.hardMode,
9727	      evil: w.crimson ? 'crimson' : 'corrupt',
9728	      anyBoss,
9729	      hasWorldSurface: w.groundLevel > 50,  // Main.isThereAWorldSurface（Main.cs:2861）
9730	    }, seededRng((w.seed ^ Math.imul(w.clock.dayCount, 0x9E3779B1)) | 0));
9731	  }
9732	
9733	  /** 「任务」按钮（NPCInteractions.AnglerQuest.Interact → Main.NPCChatText_DoAnglerQuest
9734	   *  Main.cs:39654-39690）1:1：交付门 → 扣鱼 → 计数++ → 四段奖励 → 台词替换 */
9735	  anglerQuestTurnIn(): void {
9736	    const w = this.world;
9737	    const p = this.player;
9738	    const npc = this.dialogNpc;
9739	    if (!npc) return;
9740	    let done = false;
9741	    // :39659 门：今日任务未交 且 本玩家今日未交（多人各限一次）
9742	    if (!w.anglerQuestFinished && !w.anglerWhoFinishedToday.includes(this.playerName())) {
9743	      const questId = this.anglerQuestItemId();
9744	      const internal = VI_ID(questId);
9745	      const slot = p.inv.slots.findIndex((s) => s && s.id === internal && s.stack > 0);  // FindItem :39661
9746	      if (slot >= 0) {
9747	        p.inv.removeAt(slot, 1);            // :39664-39668（stack 归零即清槽）
9748	        done = true;
9749	        p.anglerQuestsFinished++;           // :39671
9750	        this.grantAnglerRewards(npc, questId);   // GetAnglerReward :39672
9751	      }
9752	    }
9753	    // 台词（Lang.AnglerQuestChat Lang.cs:430-443）：成功 → TurnIn 池；今日已交/未带鱼 →
9754	    // NoQuest 池；否则 Quest_<物品内名>（item 内名 = vi_<id>_<Name> 键尾段）
9755	    let chat: string;
9756	    if (done) {
9757	      chat = Lang.specialText('AnglerQuestText.TurnIn_') ?? Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.AnglerTurnIn');
9758	      w.anglerQuestFinished = true;         // :39678
9759	      w.anglerWhoFinishedToday.push(this.playerName());   // :39685（单人即本地名）
9760	    } else if (w.anglerQuestFinished) {
9761	      chat = Lang.specialText('AnglerQuestText.NoQuest_') ?? Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.AnglerNoQuest');
9762	    } else {
9763	      const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[this.anglerQuestItemId()] ?? '';
9764	      const internal = key.split('_')[2] ?? '';
9765	      chat = Lang.text(`AnglerQuestText.Quest_${internal}`)
9766	        || Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.AnglerNoQuest');
9767	    }
9768	    this.sfx.play('menuTick');
9769	    this.refreshNpcDialog(npc, chat);
9770	    this.cb.onInventoryChanged?.();
9771	  }
9772	
9773	  /** Player.GetAnglerReward（Player.cs:55351-55361）四段奖励落物：主奖励/装饰/金币/鱼饵
9774	   *  全部经 rollAnglerRewards 纯函数结算（数值逐条行号见 data/vanillaFishing.ts），
9775	   *  在渔夫脚旁生成（QuickSpawnItem → 本仓 spawnDrop） */
9776	  private grantAnglerRewards(npc: TownNPC, questItemType: number): void {
9777	    const p = this.player;
9778	    // DropAnglerAccByMissing 的持有集（Player.cs:55647-55685：inventory+armor+bank×4+loadouts）
9779	    const owned = new Set<number>();
9780	    const collect = (it: { id: number; stack: number } | null) => {
9781	      const key = it ? ITEM_DEFS[it.id]?.key : undefined;
9782	      const m = key ? /^vi_(\d+)_/.exec(key) : null;
9783	      if (m) owned.add(+m[1]);
9784	    };
9785	    p.inv.slots.forEach(collect);
9786	    p.inv.armor?.forEach(collect);
9787	    p.banks?.forEach((b) => b?.forEach(collect));
9788	    p.inv.loadouts?.forEach((l) => { l?.armor.forEach(collect); });
9789	    for (const g of rollAnglerRewards({
9790	      questsDone: p.anglerQuestsFinished,
9791	      questItemType,
9792	      hardMode: !!this.world.flags.hardMode,
9793	      ownedTypes: owned,
9794	    })) {
9795	      const key = VI(g.id);
9796	      if (key) this.spawnDrop(npc.cx, npc.cy - 8, key, g.stack, 0, -2);
9797	    }
9798	  }
9799	
9800	  /** 城镇 NPC 社交表情（原版 ai[0]=7/19 相邻互发 EmoteBubble 的简化切片，NPC.cs:69936）：
9801	   *  白天、空闲、地面上的 NPC 与 6 格内另一 NPC 配对，低频随机互发表情 */
9802	  private emoteCooldown = 0;
9803	  private tickNpcEmotes(): void {
9804	    if (this.world.clock.isDay && --this.emoteCooldown <= 0) {
9805	      this.emoteCooldown = 600; // 每 10s 窗口一次
9806	      const npcs = this.entities.npcs.filter(
9807	        (n) => n instanceof TownNPC && !n.dead && !n.bound && !n.talking) as TownNPC[];
9808	      if (npcs.length >= 2) {
9809	        for (let i = 0; i < npcs.length - 1; i++) {
9810	          const a = npcs[i];
9811	          for (let j = i + 1; j < npcs.length; j++) {
9812	            const b = npcs[j];
9813	            if (Math.hypot(a.cx - b.cx, a.cy - b.cy) > 6 * TILE) continue;
9814	            // 表情池（EmoteID 实号：25 群系/90 道具/100 火/87 疑惑/89 睡意——原版社交段同源）
9815	            const pool = [25, 90, 100, 87, 89];
9816	            spawnEmote(a, pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)], 180);
9817	            spawnEmote(b, pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)], 180);
9818	            a.facing = b.cx < a.cx ? -1 : 1;  // 面向对方（原版社交状态朝向）
9819	            b.facing = a.cx < b.cx ? -1 : 1;
9820	            // 原版进入 ai[0]=7 社交态即停走；近似：重置游走计时让双方站定片刻
9821	            a.wanderT = Math.max(a.wanderT, 220); b.wanderT = Math.max(b.wanderT, 220);
9822	            a.vx *= 0.3; b.vx *= 0.3;
9823	            return;
9824	          }
9825	        }
9826	      }
9827	      // 空闲随机冒泡(原版散落情境 NewBubble 的可见子集;emote 均匀 0..120,
9828	      // 240t)——单 NPC 也冒泡,喂同一 EmoteBubble 模块(TownNPC 内联版已退役)
9829	      if (npcs.length >= 1 && Math.random() < 0.5) {
9830	        const n = npcs[Math.floor(Math.random() * npcs.length)];
9831	        spawnEmote(n, Math.floor(Math.random() * 121), 240);
9832	      }
9833	    }
9834	  }

9841	  }
```

```
	public void SetTalkNPC(int npcIndex)
	{
		talkNPC = npcIndex;
		if (Main.netMode != 1 && npcIndex >= 0 && npcIndex < Main.maxNPCs)
		{
			Main.BestiaryTracker.Chats.RegisterChatStartWith(Main.npc[npcIndex]);
		}
		if (talkNPC == -1)
		{
			currentShoppingSettings = ShoppingSettings.NotInShop;
		}
		else
		{
			currentShoppingSettings = Main.ShopHelper.GetShoppingSettings(this, Main.npc[talkNPC]);
		}
		if (currentShoppingSettings.PriceAdjustment <= 0.82f)
		{
			AchievementsHelper.HandleSpecialEvent(this, 20);
		}
		if (whoAmI == Main.myPlayer)
		{
			Main.npcChatPortrait = null;
			if (npcIndex >= 0 && npcIndex < Main.maxNPCs && NPCID.Sets.NPCPortraits.TryGetValue(Main.npc[npcIndex].type, out var value))
			{
				Main.npcChatPortrait = value;
				Main.DoNPCPortraitHop();
			}
		}
	}

	public void SetItemTime(int frames)
	{
		itemTime = frames;
		itemTimeMax = frames;
	}
```

```
		Register(new Actions.ReportHappiness());
		Register(new Actions.RequestHome());
	}

	private static void Shop(int npcType, int shopIndex, string customTextKey = null)
	{
		Register(new Actions.OpenShop(npcType, shopIndex, customTextKey));
	}

	private static void Register(NPCInteraction interaction)
	{
		All.Add(interaction);
	}
}
```

```
295		private List<NPC> GetNearbyResidentNPCs(NPC npc, out int npcsWithinHouse, out int npcsWithinVillage)
296		{
297			List<NPC> list = new List<NPC>();
298			npcsWithinHouse = 0;
299			npcsWithinVillage = 0;
300			Vector2 value = new Vector2(npc.homeTileX, npc.homeTileY);
301			if (npc.homeless)
302			{
303				value = new Vector2(npc.Center.X / 16f, npc.Center.Y / 16f);
304			}
305			for (int i = 0; i < Main.maxNPCs; i++)
306			{
307				if (i == npc.whoAmI)
308				{
309					continue;
310				}
311				NPC nPC = Main.npc[i];
312				if (nPC.active && nPC.townNPC && !IsNotReallyTownNPC(nPC) && !WorldGen.TownManager.CanNPCsLiveWithEachOther_ShopHelper(npc, nPC))
313				{
314					Vector2 value2 = new Vector2(nPC.homeTileX, nPC.homeTileY);
315					if (nPC.homeless)
316					{
317						value2 = nPC.Center / 16f;
318					}
319					float num = Vector2.Distance(value, value2);
320					if (num < 25f)
321					{
322						list.Add(nPC);
323						npcsWithinHouse++;
324					}
325					else if (num < 120f)
326					{
327						npcsWithinVillage++;
328					}
329				}
330			}
331			return list;
332		}
333	
334		private bool RuinMoodIfHomeless(NPC npc)
335		{
336			if (npc.homeless)
337			{
338				AddHappinessReportText("NoHome");
339			}
340			return npc.homeless;
341		}
342	
343		private bool IsFarFromHome(NPC npc)
344		{
345			Vector2 value = new Vector2(npc.homeTileX, npc.homeTileY);
346			Vector2 value2 = new Vector2(npc.Center.X / 16f, npc.Center.Y / 16f);
347			if (Vector2.Distance(value, value2) > 120f)
348			{
349				AddHappinessReportText("FarFromHome");
350				return true;
351			}
352			return false;
353		}
354	
355		private bool IsPlayerInEvilBiomes(Player player)
356		{
357			for (int i = 0; i < _dangerousBiomes.Length; i++)
358			{
359				AShoppingBiome aShoppingBiome = _dangerousBiomes[i];
360				if (aShoppingBiome.IsInBiome(player))
361				{
362					AddHappinessReportText("HateBiome", new
363					{
364						BiomeName = BiomeNameByKey(aShoppingBiome.NameKey)
365					});
366					return true;
367				}
368			}
369			return false;
370		}
371	
372		private bool IsNotReallyTownNPC(NPC npc)
373		{
374			int type = npc.type;
375			if (type == 37 || type == 368 || type == 453)
376			{
377				return true;
378			}
379			return false;
380		}
381	}
```

```
35	  valueToCoins(value: number): string {
36	    let rest = Math.max(0, Math.floor(value));

39	    while (rest >= 10000) { rest -= 10000; gold++; }
40	    while (rest >= 100) { rest -= 100; silver++; }

44	    if (gold > 0) text += `${gold} ${Lang.text('Currency.Gold').toLowerCase()} `;
45	    if (silver > 0) text += `${silver} ${Lang.text('Currency.Silver').toLowerCase()} `;
46	    if (copper > 0) text += `${copper} ${Lang.text('Currency.Copper').toLowerCase()} `;
47	    if (text.length > 0) text = text.slice(0, -1);

92	  /** 物品名(本仓库 item key)。解析链：

96	   *  ② Mods.SandboxWorld.ItemName.<PascalKey>（l10n-custom 自有物品——原版不存在的发明物）
97	   *  ③ ItemDef.name 硬编码兜底 */
98	  itemNameByKey(key: string): string {
99	    const vi = key.match(/^vi_(\d+)_/);
100	    const id = vi ? Number(vi[1]) : ITEM_KEY_TO_ID[key];
101	    if (id != null) {
102	      const n = Lang.itemName(id);
103	      if (n) return n;
104	      // id-maps 兜底（l10n 缺译的少数 id；zh 系取 zh 其余取 en）
105	      const isZh = (languageManager.activeCulture?.name ?? '').startsWith('zh');

107	      if (fb) return fb;
108	    }
109	    if (key.startsWith('place_v_')) {

111	      const sheet = tileKey !== undefined ? TILE_DEFS[TILE_BY_KEY[tileKey]]?.vanilla?.sheet : undefined;
112	      if (sheet !== undefined) {
113	        const n = Lang.tileName(sheet);
114	        if (n) return n;
115	      }
116	    }
117	    const custom = `Mods.SandboxWorld.ItemName.${key.replace(/(^|_)([a-z0-9])/g, (_, p, c) => (p ? c.toUpperCase() : c.toUpperCase()))}`;
118	    if (Lang.has(custom)) return Lang.text(custom);
119	    return ITEM_DEFS[ITEM_BY_KEY[key]]?.name || key;
```

```
1	// 语言包运行时核心。1:1 对照 Terarria1456/Terraria.Localization/LanguageManager.cs。
2	//
3	// 与原版的差异(有意为之,构建管线 scripts/build-l10n.mjs 已承担):
4	// - 原版 ReloadLanguage(L102)的"值重置为 key → 加载 en-US → 加载目标语言"回退,
5	//   在构建期完成(目标语言包已按 en 基线 overlay 缺失键),运行时单文件单请求。
6	// - {$Key} 跨键复制(L158)与 {?Cond} 条件标记也已构建期处理。
7	// - 本文件保留 loadPackJson 顺序加载语义(测试可连续注入模拟原版双载)。
8	
9	import { DEFAULT_CULTURE_NAME, GameCulture, normalizeLang } from './GameCulture';
10	
11	type Pack = Record<string, Record<string, string>>; // { [category]: { [key]: value } }
12	
13	export interface LangCultureInfo {
14	  name: string;
15	  legacyId: number;
16	  displayNameKey: string;
17	}
18	
19	/** 语言包数据表 + 类别索引(共用 full key 字符串引用,不复制内存) */
20	interface LoadedPack {
21	  texts: Map<string, string>;                  // full key = category + '.' + key
22	  groupedKeys: Map<string, string[]>;          // category → full keys(保插入序)
23	  variations: Map<string, Map<string, string>>; // base key → { variant: value }(LanguageManager.cs:300)
24	}
25	
26	const MAX_PACK_CACHE = 2; // LRU 上限(性能审计:语言包 ~1MB/份,不无限缓存)
27	
28	function buildTables(pack: Pack): LoadedPack {
29	  const texts = new Map<string, string>();
30	  const groupedKeys = new Map<string, string[]>();
31	  const variations = new Map<string, Map<string, string>>();
32	  for (const [cat, entries] of Object.entries(pack)) {
33	    const list: string[] = [];
34	    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(entries)) {
35	      const fullKey = `${cat}.${key}`;
36	      // $ 变体键(如 ItemName.Chair$Gender)入变体表,不进正文(LanguageManager.cs:303-317)
37	      const dollar = key.indexOf('$');
38	      if (dollar > 0) {
39	        const base = `${cat}.${key.slice(0, dollar)}`;
40	        let variants = variations.get(base);
41	        if (!variants) { variants = new Map(); variations.set(base, variants); }
42	        variants.set(key.slice(dollar + 1), value);
43	        continue;
44	      }
45	      texts.set(fullKey, value);
46	      list.push(fullKey);
47	    }
48	    groupedKeys.set(cat, list);
49	  }
50	  return { texts, groupedKeys, variations };
51	}
52	
53	class LanguageManager {
54	  activeCulture: LangCultureInfo | null = null;
55	  cultures: LangCultureInfo[] = [];            // 来自 public/l10n/index.json(设置面板数据源)
56	  private pack: LoadedPack = { texts: new Map(), groupedKeys: new Map(), variations: new Map() };
57	  private packCache = new Map<string, LoadedPack>(); // name → LoadedPack,Map 序即 LRU 序
58	  private listeners: Array<() => void> = [];
59	  private warned = new Set<string>();
60	  loaded = false;
61	
62	  /**
63	   * 启动初始化:加载语言列表 + 当前语言包。
64	   * lang 未指定时用 DEFAULT_LANG(zh-Hans);非法值归一到 en-US。
65	   */
66	  async init(lang?: string | number): Promise<boolean> {
67	    await this.loadIndex();
68	    return this.setLanguage(lang ?? 'zh-Hans');
69	  }
70	
71	  private async loadIndex() {
72	    if (this.cultures.length) return;
73	    try {
74	      const r = await fetch('l10n/index.json');
75	      if (r.ok) this.cultures = await r.json();
76	    } catch { /* 列表缺失时仍可用 GameCulture 静态表 */
77	      if (!this.cultures.length) {
78	        const { CULTURES } = await import('./GameCulture');
79	        this.cultures = CULTURES;
80	      }
81	    }
82	  }
83	
84	  /** 切换语言(culture 对象 | culture 名 | legacyId;LanguageManager.cs:54/60/92) */
85	  async setLanguage(culture: LangCultureInfo | string | number): Promise<boolean> {
86	    const name = typeof culture === 'object' ? culture.name : normalizeLang(culture);
87	    if (this.activeCulture?.name === name && this.loaded) return true;
88	    const tables = await this.fetchPack(name);
89	    if (!tables) return false;
90	    this.pack = tables;
91	    this.activeCulture = this.cultures.find((c) => c.name === name) ?? { name, legacyId: 0, displayNameKey: '' };
92	    this.loaded = true;
93	    this.warned.clear();
94	    this.emit();
95	    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.dispatchEvent(new Event('sw:langchange'));
96	    return true;
97	  }
98	
99	  private async fetchPack(name: string): Promise<LoadedPack | null> {
100	    const cached = this.packCache.get(name);
101	    if (cached) {
102	      this.packCache.delete(name);
103	      this.packCache.set(name, cached); // 刷新 LRU 序
104	      return cached;
105	    }
106	    try {
107	      const r = await fetch(`l10n/${name}.json`);
108	      if (!r.ok) return null;
109	      const pack = (await r.json()) as Pack;
110	      const tables = buildTables(pack);
111	      this.packCache.set(name, tables);
112	      while (this.packCache.size > MAX_PACK_CACHE) {
113	        const oldest = this.packCache.keys().next().value as string;
114	        if (oldest === name) break;
115	        this.packCache.delete(oldest);
116	      }
117	      return tables;
118	    } catch {
119	      return null;
120	    }
121	  }
122	
123	  /** 测试注入入口(对标 LoadLanguageFromFileTextJson:可连续调用模拟原版顺序加载覆盖) */
124	  loadPackJson(pack: Pack) {
125	    for (const [cat, entries] of Object.entries(pack)) {
126	      const list = this.pack.groupedKeys.get(cat) ?? [];
127	      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(entries)) {
128	        const fullKey = `${cat}.${key}`;
129	        const dollar = key.indexOf('$');
130	        if (dollar > 0) {
131	          const base = `${cat}.${key.slice(0, dollar)}`;
132	          let variants = this.pack.variations.get(base);
133	          if (!variants) { variants = new Map(); this.pack.variations.set(base, variants); }
134	          variants.set(key.slice(dollar + 1), value);
135	          continue;
136	        }
137	        if (!this.pack.texts.has(fullKey)) list.push(fullKey);
138	        this.pack.texts.set(fullKey, value);
139	      }
140	      this.pack.groupedKeys.set(cat, list);
141	    }
142	  }
143	
144	  /** 重置为测试初始态(含 pack 缓存) */
145	  resetForTest() {
146	    this.pack = { texts: new Map(), groupedKeys: new Map(), variations: new Map() };
147	    this.activeCulture = null;
148	    this.loaded = false;
149	    this.warned.clear();
150	    this.packCache.clear();
151	  }
152	
153	  exists(key: string): boolean {
154	    return this.pack.texts.has(key);
155	  }
156	
157	  /** 取原文(不做 {0} 参数替换)。缺失返回 key 本身(LanguageManager.cs:446-453) */
158	  getText(key: string): string {
159	    const v = this.pack.texts.get(key);
160	    if (v === undefined) {
161	      if (!this.warned.has(key)) { this.warned.add(key); console.warn(`[Lang] 缺失 key: ${key}`); }
162	      return key;
163	    }
164	    return v;
165	  }
166	
167	  /** 取文案并做 {0}/{1} 位置参数替换(LanguageManager.cs:455-498 GetTextValue) */
168	  getTextValue(key: string, ...args: (string | number)[]): string {
169	    let s = this.getText(key);
170	    for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) s = s.split(`{${i}}`).join(String(args[i]));
171	    return s;
172	  }
173	
174	  /** $ 变体查询(对标 TryGetVariation,LanguageManager.cs:509) */
175	  tryGetVariation(baseKey: string, variant: string): string | null {
176	    return this.pack.variations.get(baseKey)?.get(variant) ?? null;
177	  }
178	
179	  /** 类别内取模循环取值(对标 IndexedFromCategory,LanguageManager.cs:431) */
180	  indexedFromCategory(category: string, index: number): string | null {
181	    const list = this.pack.groupedKeys.get(category);
182	    if (!list || !list.length) return null;
183	    const i = ((index % list.length) + list.length) % list.length;
184	    return this.pack.texts.get(list[i]) ?? null;
185	  }
186	
187	  /** 类别内随机(对标 RandomFromCategory,LanguageManager.cs:422) */
188	  randomFromCategory(category: string, rng: () => number = Math.random): string | null {
189	    const list = this.pack.groupedKeys.get(category);
190	    if (!list || !list.length) return null;
191	    return this.pack.texts.get(list[Math.floor(rng() * list.length)]) ?? null;
192	  }
193	
194	  /** Lang.CreateDialogFilter(startsWith)（Lang.cs:425-428）+ Language.SelectRandom：
195	   *  类目内全键前缀匹配后随机一条（NPC.HasSpecialEventText 的
196	   *  "XxxSpecialText.Yyy" 族——键形如 MerchantSpecialText.Rain1/Rain2） */
197	  randomFromPrefix(prefix: string, rng: () => number = Math.random): string | null {
198	    const dot = prefix.indexOf('.');
199	    if (dot <= 0) return null;
200	    const list = this.pack.groupedKeys.get(prefix.slice(0, dot));
201	    if (!list || !list.length) return null;
202	    const hits = list.filter((k) => k.startsWith(prefix));
203	    if (!hits.length) return null;
204	    return this.pack.texts.get(hits[Math.floor(rng() * hits.length)]) ?? null;
205	  }
206	
207	  categorySize(category: string): number {
208	    return this.pack.groupedKeys.get(category)?.length ?? 0;
209	  }
210	
211	  /** OnLanguageChanged(LanguageManager.cs:38)。返回退订函数 */
212	  onChange(fn: () => void): () => void {
213	    this.listeners.push(fn);
214	    return () => { this.listeners = this.listeners.filter((f) => f !== fn); };
215	  }
216	
217	  private emit() {
218	    for (const fn of this.listeners) fn();
219	  }
220	}
```

```
60	    skinVariant: 0,
61	    ...structuredClone(DEFAULT_COLORS),
62	    difficulty: 0,
63	  };
64	}

66	/** 旧数据/损坏数据补默认；颜色钳到 0-255 */
67	export function validateAppearance(a: Partial<Appearance> | null | undefined): Appearance {
68	  const d = defaultAppearance();
69	  if (!a) return d;
70	  const c = (v: unknown, fallback: RGBColor): RGBColor => {
71	    if (typeof v !== 'object' || v === null) return fallback;
72	    const o = v as Partial<RGBColor>;
73	    const cl = (n: unknown) => Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(Number(n) || 0)));
74	    return { r: cl(o.r), g: cl(o.g), b: cl(o.b) };
75	  };
76	  return {
77	    name: typeof a.name === 'string' ? a.name.slice(0, 20) : d.name,
78	    // 1.4.5.6 保护（Player.cs:54209）：hair >= 228 → 0
79	    hair: clampInt(a.hair, 0, HAIR_COUNT - 1, d.hair),
80	    skinVariant: clampInt(a.skinVariant, 0, 11, d.skinVariant),
81	    hairColor: c(a.hairColor, d.hairColor),
82	    skinColor: c(a.skinColor, d.skinColor),
83	    eyeColor: c(a.eyeColor, d.eyeColor),
84	    shirtColor: c(a.shirtColor, d.shirtColor),
85	    undershirtColor: c(a.undershirtColor, d.undershirtColor),
86	    pantsColor: c(a.pantsColor, d.pantsColor),
87	    shoeColor: c(a.shoeColor, d.shoeColor),
88	    difficulty: ([0, 1, 2, 3] as number[]).includes(a.difficulty as number) ? (a.difficulty as Difficulty) : 0,
89	    hardcoreDead: a.hardcoreDead === true ? true : undefined,   // 硬核消亡标记（旧数据缺省未消亡）
90	  };
91	}

103	/** 角色创建可用发型白名单（HairstyleUnlocksHelper.RebuildList：0-50 + 136-144,147-151,154,155,157,158,161
104	 *  + 51-122 + 134,135,146,152,153,156,159,165,160 + 166-227） */
105	export const CHAR_CREATION_HAIRS: number[] = (() => {
106	  const list: number[] = [];
107	  for (let i = 0; i <= 50; i++) list.push(i);
108	  list.push(136, 137, 138, 139, 140, 141, 142, 143, 144, 147, 148, 149, 150, 151, 154, 155, 157, 158, 161);
109	  for (let i = 51; i <= 122; i++) list.push(i);
110	  list.push(134, 135, 146, 152, 153, 156, 159, 165, 160);
111	  for (let i = 166; i < HAIR_COUNT; i++) list.push(i);
112	  return list;
113	})();

117	  43, 44, 45, 46, 47, 48, 50, 55, 64, 65, 66, 67, 68, 69, 70, 71, 72, 73, 78, 79, 80, 81, 83, 84, 85, 86, 87,
118	  89, 90, 91, 92, 94, 95, 97, 99, 101, 103, 106, 107, 112, 123, 125, 132, 133, 134, 143, 145, 146, 162, 164]);

120	/** 后发层判定（Player.GetHairSettings:16771，0 基 id） */
121	export function backHairDraw(hair: number): boolean {
122	  if (hair === 133 || hair === 134 || hair === 146 || hair === 162 || hair === 6) return true;
123	  return hair > 50 && (hair < 56 || hair > 63) && (hair < 74 || hair > 77) && (hair < 88 || hair > 89)
124	    && hair !== 94 && hair !== 100 && hair !== 104 && hair !== 112 && hair < 116;
125	}

127	/** 随机外观（对齐 1.4.5.6 CreateCharacter：从白名单随机发型，
128	 *  性别由发型判定表推导（FEMALE_HAIRS），变体从对应性别列表取） */
129	export function randomAppearance(name = ''): Appearance {
130	  const a = defaultAppearance(name);
131	  a.hair = CHAR_CREATION_HAIRS[Math.floor(Math.random() * CHAR_CREATION_HAIRS.length)];
132	  const pool = FEMALE_HAIRS.has(a.hair) ? FEMALE_VARIANTS : MALE_VARIANTS;
133	  a.skinVariant = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)];
134	  const hsl = (h: number, s: number, l: number) => {
135	    const [r, g, b] = hslToRgb(h / 360, s, l);
136	    return { r: Math.round(r * 255), g: Math.round(g * 255), b: Math.round(b * 255) };
137	  };
138	  a.skinColor = hsl(20 + Math.random() * 20, 0.45, 0.55 + Math.random() * 0.25);
139	  a.hairColor = hsl(Math.random() * 360, 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.5, 0.2 + Math.random() * 0.4);
140	  a.eyeColor = hsl(Math.random() * 360, 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.4, 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.3);
141	  a.shirtColor = hsl(Math.random() * 360, 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.4, 0.45 + Math.random() * 0.2);
142	  a.undershirtColor = hsl(Math.random() * 360, 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.4, 0.45 + Math.random() * 0.2);
143	  a.pantsColor = hsl(Math.random() * 360, 0.25 + Math.random() * 0.35, 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.3);
144	  a.shoeColor = hsl(Math.random() * 360, 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.4, 0.25 + Math.random() * 0.25);
145	  a.difficulty = 0;
146	  return a;
147	}
148	
149	function clampInt(v: unknown, min: number, max: number, fallback: number): number {
150	  const n = Math.round(Number(v));
151	  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return fallback;
152	  return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, n));
153	}
154	
155	/** HSL → RGB（0-1 输入/输出） */
156	export function hslToRgb(h: number, s: number, l: number): [number, number, number] {
157	  h = ((h % 1) + 1) % 1;
158	  const f = (n: number) => {
159	    const k = (n + h * 12) % 12;
160	    return l - s * Math.min(l, 1 - l) * Math.max(-1, Math.min(k - 3, 9 - k, 1));
161	  };
162	  return [f(0), f(8), f(4)];
163	}
164	
165	export function rgbToHsl(r: number, g: number, b: number): [number, number, number] {
166	  const rr = r / 255, gg = g / 255, bb = b / 255;
167	  const max = Math.max(rr, gg, bb), min = Math.min(rr, gg, bb);
168	  const l = (max + min) / 2;
169	  let h = 0, s = 0;
170	  if (max !== min) {
171	    const d = max - min;
172	    s = l > 0.5 ? d / (2 - max - min) : d / (max + min);
173	    if (max === rr) h = ((gg - bb) / d + (gg < bb ? 6 : 0)) / 6;
174	    else if (max === gg) h = ((bb - rr) / d + 2) / 6;
175	    else h = ((rr - gg) / d + 4) / 6;
176	  }
177	  return [h, s, l];
178	}
179
```

```
97	export function furnitureAnchor(st: FurnitureStoreView, kind: FurnitureKind, x: number, y: number): [number, number] {
98	  const i = st.idx(x, y);

100	  switch (kind) {
101	    case 'item_frame': return [x - Math.floor((fx % 36) / 18), y - Math.floor((fy % 36) / 18)];
102	    case 'weapon_rack': return [x - Math.floor((fx % 54) / 18), y - Math.floor((fy % 54) / 18)];
103	    case 'food_platter': return [x, y];
104	    case 'display_jar': return [x, y - Math.floor((fy % 36) / 18)];
105	    case 'display_doll': return [x - (fx % 36 !== 0 ? 1 : 0), y - Math.floor(fy / 18)];
106	    case 'hat_rack': return [x - Math.floor((fx % 54) / 18), y - Math.floor(fy / 18)];
107	  }
108	}

200	  return itemFuncOfVid(vid)?.holdStyle ?? 0;
201	}
202	
203	/** Item.fishingPole（itemfunc 表，9 支钓竿） */
204	function vanillaFishingPole(vid: number): number {
205	  return itemFuncOfVid(vid)?.fishingPole ?? 0;
206	}
207	
208	/** Item.headSlot/bodySlot/legSlot > 0（vanilla-itemstats.json s 字段，Item.cs 赋值即归属） */
209	function vanillaArmorPiece(vid: number): 'h' | 'b' | 'l' | null {
210	  return vanillaStatOfVid(vid)?.s ?? null;
211	}
212	
213	/** Item.accessory（含社交配饰；vanilla-itemstats.json acc 字段存在即 true） */

215	  return vanillaStatOfVid(vid)?.acc !== undefined;
216	}
217	
218	/** Item.mountType >= 0（vanilla-equip.json mount/cart 两组 id，Item.cs mountType 赋值） */
219	function vanillaMountType(vid: number): number {
220	  const k = equipKindOfVid(vid);
221	  return k === 'mount' || k === 'cart' ? 0 : -1;
222	}
223	
224	/** Item.wingSlot > 0（vanilla-itemstats.json wing 字段） */
225	function vanillaWingSlot(vid: number): number {
226	  return vanillaStatOfVid(vid)?.wing ?? 0;
227	}
228	
229	/** TEItemFrame.FitsItemFrame（TEItemFrame.cs:170-173）/ TEDeadCellsDisplayJar.FitsJar（:126-129）：
230	 *  任何 stack>0 物品均可 */
231	export function fitsItemFrame(vid: number): boolean {
232	  return vid > 0;
233	}
234	
235	/** TEWeaponsRack.FitsWeaponFrame（TEWeaponsRack.cs:182-193）：
236	 *  !IsAir && (fishingPole>0 || Sets.CanBePlacedOnWeaponRacks) → true；
237	 *  否则 damage>0 && useStyle!=0 → stack>0 */
238	export function fitsWeaponRack(vid: number): boolean {
239	  if (vid <= 0) return false;
240	  if (vanillaFishingPole(vid) > 0 || VANILLA_WEAPON_RACK_EXTRA.has(vid)) return true;
241	  if (vanillaDamage(vid) > 0 && vanillaUseStyle(vid) !== 0) return true;
242	  return false;
243	}
244	
245	/** TEFoodPlatter.FitsFoodPlatter（TEFoodPlatter.cs:112-119）：stack>0 且 Sets.IsFood */
246	export function fitsFoodPlatter(vid: number): boolean {
247	  return vid > 0 && VANILLA_IS_FOOD.has(vid);
248	}
249	
250	/** TEHatRack.CanQuickSwapIntoHatRack（TEHatRack.cs:331-334）：headSlot > 0 */
251	export function fitsHatRack(vid: number): boolean {
252	  return vanillaArmorPiece(vid) === 'h';
253	}
254	
255	/** TEDisplayDoll.AcceptedInWeaponSlot（TEDisplayDoll.cs:673-679）：
256	 *  useStyle==0 || mountType!=-1 → holdStyle!=0；否则 true */
257	export function acceptedInWeaponSlot(vid: number): boolean {
258	  if (vanillaUseStyle(vid) === 0 || vanillaMountType(vid) !== -1) return vanillaHoldStyle(vid) !== 0;
259	  return true;
260	}
261	
262	/** TEDisplayDoll.CanQuickSwapIntoDisplayDoll（TEDisplayDoll.cs:664-671） */
263	export function fitsDisplayDoll(vid: number): boolean {
264	  if (vanillaArmorPiece(vid) || vanillaIsAccessory(vid) || vanillaMountType(vid) >= 0) return true;
265	  return acceptedInWeaponSlot(vid);
266	}
267	
268	/** 类别 → 放置判定分发（Game 侧单入口） */
269	export function fitsFurniture(kind: FurnitureKind, vid: number): boolean {
270	  switch (kind) {
271	    case 'item_frame': case 'display_jar': return fitsItemFrame(vid);
272	    case 'weapon_rack': return fitsWeaponRack(vid);
273	    case 'food_platter': return fitsFoodPlatter(vid);
274	    case 'hat_rack': return fitsHatRack(vid);
275	    case 'display_doll': return fitsDisplayDoll(vid);
276	  }
277	}
278	
279	/**
280	 * 模特快速换装目标槽（TEDisplayDoll.TryFitting :680-725 的槽位选择段）：
281	 *  headSlot>0 → 0 / bodySlot>0 → 1 / legSlot>0 → 2 / accessory → 配饰冲突槽或
282	 *  3-7 首空（GetAccessoryTargetSlot :727-738 + ItemSlot.HasIncompatibleAccessory
283	 *  ItemSlot.cs:3208-3206：同 id 或双翅膀不可共存） / mountType>=0 → 8 /
284	 *  AcceptedInWeaponSlot → misc(9)；不可放 → -1
285	 */
286	export function dollTargetSlot(rec: FurnitureItemRecord, vid: number): number {
287	  const piece = vanillaArmorPiece(vid);
288	  if (piece === 'h') return 0;
289	  if (piece === 'b') return 1;
290	  if (piece === 'l') return 2;
291	  if (vanillaIsAccessory(vid)) return dollAccessoryTargetSlot(rec, vid);
292	  if (vanillaMountType(vid) >= 0) return 8;
293	  if (acceptedInWeaponSlot(vid)) return 9;
294	  return -1;
295	}
296	
297	/** GetAccessoryTargetSlot（TEDisplayDoll.cs:727-738）：HasIncompatibleAccessory
298	 *  （ItemSlot.cs:3208-3220 + CanEquipBothAccessories :3195-3206）命中 → 冲突槽；
299	 *  否则 3..7 首空；全满 → 3 */
300	function dollAccessoryTargetSlot(rec: FurnitureItemRecord, vid: number): number {
301	  for (let i = 3; i <= 7; i++) {
302	    const cur = rec.slots[i];
303	    if (cur && (cur.id === vid || (vanillaWingSlot(cur.id) > 0 && vanillaWingSlot(vid) > 0))) return i;
304	  }
305	  for (let i = 3; i <= 7; i++) {
306	    if (!rec.slots[i]) return i;
307	  }
308	  return 3;
309	}
310	
311	// ============================================================================
312	// 交互语义（单槽族：放入/替换/弹出；原版 TryPlacing/OnPlayerInteraction）
313	// ============================================================================
314	
315	/**
316	 * 单槽族放入（TEItemFrame/TEWeaponsRack/TEFoodPlatter/TEDeadCellsDisplayJar 的
317	 * TryPlacing :83-100：已有物先弹出（返回 replaced 由调用方掉落），再写入新物 stack=1）。
318	 * @returns replaced 被替换出的旧物（null=原本为空）；fits=false 时不变更返回 null 且 ok=false
319	 */
320	export function furniturePlaceSingle(
321	  records: FurnitureItemRecord[], kind: FurnitureKind, ax: number, ay: number,
322	  item: { id: number; stack: number; prefix?: number }, vid: number,
323	): { ok: boolean; replaced: FurnitureSlot } {
324	  if (!fitsFurniture(kind, vid)) return { ok: false, replaced: null };
325	  const rec = getOrCreateFurniture(records, kind, ax, ay);
326	  const replaced = rec.slots[0];
327	  rec.slots[0] = { id: item.id, stack: 1, prefix: item.prefix };
328	  return { ok: true, replaced };
329	}
330	
331	/**
332	 * 单槽族取出（OnPlayerInteraction else 分支 :134-151 → WorldGen.KillTile(fail:true)
333	 * → KillTile_DropItems :63703-63738 提前 return：弹出存放物、方块保留不破坏）。
334	 * @returns 被弹出的物品（null=本来就空）
335	 */
336	export function furniturePopSingle(records: FurnitureItemRecord[], kind: FurnitureKind, ax: number, ay: number): FurnitureSlot {
337	  const rec = getFurniture(records, kind, ax, ay);
338	  if (!rec) return null;
339	  const item = rec.slots[0];
340	  rec.slots[0] = null;
341	  return item;
342	}
343	
344	/**
345	 * 破坏判定（WorldGen.KillTile 前置特例）：
346	 *  单槽族有物 → 'pop'（先弹物、本次不破坏，KillTile_DropItems :63703 提前 return 语义）；
347	 *  模特/帽架有物 → 'blocked'（IsBreakable=false，WorldGen.cs:62540-62560
348	 *    CheckTileBreakability2_ShouldTileSurvive：有物整件不可破坏）；
349	 *  其余 → 'break'（正常破坏，调用方清格后 removeFurnitureAt）
350	 */
351	export function furnitureTryBreak(
352	  records: FurnitureItemRecord[], kind: FurnitureKind, ax: number, ay: number,
353	): { result: 'pop' | 'blocked' | 'break'; item: FurnitureSlot } {
354	  const rec = getFurniture(records, kind, ax, ay);
355	  if (!rec || !furnitureContainsItems(rec)) return { result: 'break', item: null };
356	  switch (kind) {
357	    case 'display_doll': case 'hat_rack':
358	      return { result: 'blocked', item: null };
359	    default: {
360	      const item = rec.slots[0];
361	      rec.slots[0] = null;
362	      return { result: 'pop', item };
363	    }
364	  }
365	}
366	
367	/**
368	 * 模特放置：TryFitting 语义（TEDisplayDoll.cs:680-725）——
369	 *  目标槽已占用且手持 stack>1 → 不动（原版 return true 仅播声）；
370	 *  手持 stack>1 → 槽内放 1 个（克隆），手持 -1；
371	 *  手持 stack==1 → 与槽位整体交换（换出物回手持格，由调用方写回）。
372	 * @returns { target, swappedOut } 或 null（不可放/未动）
373	 */
374	export function dollPlace(
375	  records: FurnitureItemRecord[], ax: number, ay: number,
376	  item: { id: number; stack: number; prefix?: number }, vid: number,
377	): { target: number; swappedOut: FurnitureSlot } | null {
378	  const rec = getOrCreateFurniture(records, 'display_doll', ax, ay);
379	  const target = dollTargetSlot(rec, vid);
380	  if (target < 0) return null;
381	  return tryFitting(rec, target, item);
382	}
383	
384	/** TEHatRack.TryFitting 槽位轮转（TEHatRack.cs:342-347 + 静态 hatTargetSlot :18）：
385	 *  0/1 首空，否则用轮转位；放置后轮转指针前移（module 级静态，对表原版 static） */
386	let hatTargetSlotPtr = 0;
387	function hatRackTargetSlot(rec: FurnitureItemRecord): number {
388	  for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
389	    if (!rec.slots[i]) return i;
390	  }
391	  return hatTargetSlotPtr;
392	}
393	
394	/** 帽架放置（TEHatRack.TryFitting :337-369；CanQuickSwapIntoHatRack 门在外层 fitsFurniture） */
395	export function hatRackPlace(
396	  records: FurnitureItemRecord[], ax: number, ay: number,
397	  item: { id: number; stack: number; prefix?: number }, vid: number,
398	): { target: number; swappedOut: FurnitureSlot } | null {
399	  if (!fitsHatRack(vid)) return null;
400	  const rec = getOrCreateFurniture(records, 'hat_rack', ax, ay);
401	  const r = tryFitting(rec, hatRackTargetSlot(rec), item);
402	  if (r) hatTargetSlotPtr = r.target + 1 >= 2 ? 0 : r.target + 1; // hatTargetSlot++ 回绕（:363-367）
403	  return r;
404	}
405	
406	/** TryFitting 共用主体（TEDisplayDoll.cs:693-718 / TEHatRack.cs:350-360）：
407	 *  占位+多叠 → null；多叠 → 克隆 1 并手持 -1；单叠 → 整体交换（Utils.Swap——
408	 *  手持格收到原槽内容，空槽即空气，故置 item.stack=0 由调用方清手持格） */
409	function tryFitting(
410	  rec: FurnitureItemRecord, target: number,
411	  item: { id: number; stack: number; prefix?: number },
412	): { target: number; swappedOut: FurnitureSlot } | null {
413	  const cur = rec.slots[target];
414	  if (item.stack > 1 && cur) return null; // :693-696
415	  if (item.stack > 1) {
416	    rec.slots[target] = { id: item.id, stack: 1, prefix: item.prefix };
417	    item.stack -= 1; // :699-704 Clone（stack=1）+ item.stack--
418	    return { target, swappedOut: null };
419	  }
420	  rec.slots[target] = { id: item.id, stack: item.stack, prefix: item.prefix }; // Utils.Swap :705-709
421	  item.stack = 0; // 手持格交出整件（原槽为空 → Swap 后手持=空气）
422	  return { target, swappedOut: cur };
423	}
424	
425	/**
426	 * 多槽族取出（模特/帽架右键且手持不可放时的最小等价）：
427	 *  原版此时打开槽位 UI（TileEntity.BasicOpenCloseInteraction，TileEntity.cs:250-285）
428	 *  由玩家在面板取物；本作无该 UI，等价为按原版槽位序（模特 equip0-8→misc→dyes，
429	 *  帽架 hat0-1→dye0-1）取首个非空槽。返回 { slot, item }，槽位被清空。
430	 */
431	export function furnitureTakeFirst(records: FurnitureItemRecord[], kind: FurnitureKind, ax: number, ay: number): { slot: number; item: FurnitureSlotItem } | null {
432	  const rec = getFurniture(records, kind, ax, ay);
433	  if (!rec) return null;
434	  for (let i = 0; i < rec.slots.length; i++) {
435	    const s = rec.slots[i];
436	    if (s) {
437	      rec.slots[i] = null;
438	      return { slot: i, item: s };
439	    }
440	  }
441	  return null;
442	}
443	
444	// ============================================================================
445	// 持久化（serialize.ts furniture 段的纯换算；item id 映射由调用方注入，
446	//   与 chests 的 ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL 路径同构）
447	// ============================================================================
448	
449	/** 存档形状（SaveData.furniture；旧档缺省无） */
450	export interface FurnitureSaveEntry {
451	  x: number;
452	  y: number;
453	  kind: FurnitureKind;
454	  slots: Array<{ id: number; stack: number; prefix?: number } | null>;
455	}
456	
457	/** 运行时记录 → 存档条目（mapId = 内部 id → 磁盘稳定 id） */
458	export function furnitureToSave(records: FurnitureItemRecord[], mapId: (id: number) => number): FurnitureSaveEntry[] {
459	  return records.map((r) => ({
460	    x: r.x, y: r.y, kind: r.kind,
461	    slots: r.slots.map((s) => (s ? { id: mapId(s.id), stack: s.stack, prefix: s.prefix } : null)),
462	  }));
463	}
464	
465	/** 存档条目 → 运行时记录（mapId = 磁盘稳定 id → 内部 id；防御：未知 id 归 0 → 置空槽） */
466	export function furnitureFromSave(entries: FurnitureSaveEntry[] | undefined, mapId: (id: number) => number): FurnitureItemRecord[] {
467	  if (!entries) return [];
468	  const out: FurnitureItemRecord[] = [];
469	  for (const e of entries) {
470	    const n = furnitureSlotCount(e.kind);
471	    const slots: FurnitureSlot[] = [];
472	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
473	      const s = e.slots[i];
474	      const mapped = s ? mapId(s.id) : 0;
475	      if (s && mapped > 0) slots.push({ id: mapped, stack: s.stack, prefix: s.prefix });
476	      else slots.push(null);
477	    }
478	    out.push({ x: e.x, y: e.y, kind: e.kind, slots });
479	  }
480	  return out;
481	}
482
```

```
162	    this.panel.querySelector('[data-act=paste]')!.addEventListener('click', () => {
163	      if (!this.clipboard) return;
164	      this.appearance = { ...this.clipboard, name: this.appearance.name };
165	      this.buildContent();
166	    });
167	
168	    this.buildContent();
169	    this.loop();
170	    // Esc 返回
171	    this.escHandler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
172	      if (e.code === 'Escape') this.close(this.cb.onCancel);
173	    };
174	    window.addEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
175	    this.unsubLang = Lang.onChange(() => this.refreshTexts());
176	  }
177	
178	  private unsubLang: (() => void) | null = null;
179	
180	  /** 语言切换:静态框架文案重取 + 内容区重建(保留 appearance 与页签) */
181	  private refreshTexts() {
182	    (this.panel.querySelector('.sw-char-title') as HTMLElement).textContent = Lang.text('LegacyMenu.16');
183	    const tabText: Record<string, string> = {
184	      info: Lang.text('UI.PlayerCreateCategoryInfo'),
185	      look: Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.CharCreate.Appearance'),
186	      hair: Lang.text('UI.PlayerCreateCategoryHairStyle'),
187	      color: Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.CharCreate.Colors'),
188	    };
189	    this.panel.querySelectorAll('[data-tab]').forEach((b) => {
190	      (b as HTMLElement).textContent = tabText[(b as HTMLElement).dataset.tab!];
191	    });
192	    (this.panel.querySelector('[data-act=copy]') as HTMLElement).title = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.CharCreate.CopyTemplate');
193	    (this.panel.querySelector('[data-act=paste]') as HTMLElement).title = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.CharCreate.PasteTemplate');
194	    (this.panel.querySelector('[data-act=random]') as HTMLElement).title = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.CharCreate.Random');
195	    (this.panel.querySelector('[data-act=back]') as HTMLElement).textContent = Lang.text('UI.Back');
196	    (this.panel.querySelector('[data-act=create]') as HTMLElement).textContent = Lang.text('UI.Create');
197	    this.buildContent();
198	  }
199	
200	  private escHandler: ((e: KeyboardEvent) => void) | null = null;
201	
202	  /** 走路动画预览循环 */
203	  private loop = () => {
204	    this.time += 1 / 60;
205	    this.drawPreview();
206	    this.raf = requestAnimationFrame(this.loop);
207	  };
208	
209	  private drawPreview() {
210	    const ctx = this.previewCtx;
211	    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, 120, 150);
212	    const doll = compositePaperDoll(this.appearance);
213	    if (!doll) return;
214	    const f = dollFrame(doll, walkFrame(this.time));
215	    // 2x 放大：40×56 → 80×112，底部对齐
216	    ctx.drawImage(f.img, f.sx, f.sy, f.sw, f.sh, 20, 150 - 112, 80, 112);
217	  }
218	
219	  private close(fn: () => void) {
220	    cancelAnimationFrame(this.raf);
221	    if (this.escHandler) window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
222	    this.unsubLang?.();
223	    this.panel.remove();
224	    fn();
225	  }
226	
227	  // ================= 内容区 =================
228	
229	  private buildContent() {
230	    // 页签高亮
231	    this.panel.querySelectorAll('.sw-char-tabs button').forEach((b) => {
232	      (b as HTMLElement).classList.toggle('active', (b as HTMLElement).dataset.tab === this.tab);
233	    });
234	    this.content.innerHTML = '';
235	    this.colorInputs.clear();
236	    if (this.tab === 'info') this.buildInfo();
237	    else if (this.tab === 'look') this.buildLook();
238	    else if (this.tab === 'hair') this.buildHair();
239	    else this.buildColor();
240	  }
241	
242	  private buildInfo() {
243	    const box = document.createElement('div');
244	    box.className = 'sw-char-section';
245	    // 名字
246	    const nameRow = document.createElement('label');
247	    nameRow.className = 'sw-char-row';
248	    nameRow.innerHTML = `<span>${Lang.text('UI.PlayerNameSlot')}</span>`;
249	    const name = document.createElement('input');
250	    name.type = 'text';
251	    name.maxLength = 20;
252	    name.value = this.appearance.name;
253	    name.placeholder = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.CharCreate.NamePlaceholder');
254	    name.addEventListener('input', () => { this.appearance.name = name.value; });
255	    nameRow.appendChild(name);
256	    // 随机名（🎲）：取人类城镇 NPC 名字池并集随机（原版无玩家名字池——见 RandomText 注记）
257	    const randName = document.createElement('button');
258	    randName.textContent = '🎲';
259	    randName.title = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.WorldCreation.RandomName');
260	    randName.style.cssText = 'padding:4px 8px;cursor:pointer;';
261	    randName.addEventListener('click', () => {
262	      name.value = randomPlayerName();
263	      this.appearance.name = name.value;
264	    });
265	    nameRow.appendChild(randName);
266	    box.appendChild(nameRow);
267	    // 难度
268	    const diffTitle = document.createElement('div');
269	    diffTitle.className = 'sw-char-subtitle';
270	    diffTitle.textContent = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.CharCreate.Difficulty');
271	    box.appendChild(diffTitle);
272	    const diffRow = document.createElement('div');
273	    diffRow.className = 'sw-char-diffs';
274	    DIFFICULTY_INFO.forEach((d, i) => {
275	      const b = document.createElement('button');
276	      b.textContent = Lang.text(['UI.Softcore', 'UI.Mediumcore', 'UI.Hardcore', 'UI.Creative'][i]);
277	      b.style.color = hex(d.color);
278	      b.classList.toggle('active', this.appearance.difficulty === i);
279	      b.title = Lang.text(`Mods.SandboxWorld.CharCreate.DiffDesc.${i}`);
280	      b.addEventListener('click', () => { this.appearance.difficulty = i as Appearance['difficulty']; this.buildContent(); });
281	      diffRow.appendChild(b);
282	    });
283	    box.appendChild(diffRow);
284	    // 性别（皮肤变体男/女组）
285	    const genderTitle = document.createElement('div');
286	    genderTitle.className = 'sw-char-subtitle';
287	    genderTitle.textContent = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.CharCreate.Gender');
288	    box.appendChild(genderTitle);
289	    const gRow = document.createElement('div');
290	    gRow.className = 'sw-char-diffs';
291	    const MALE = [0, 1, 2, 3, 8, 10];
292	    const FEMALE = [4, 5, 6, 7, 9, 11];
293	    for (const [label, pool] of [['Mods.SandboxWorld.CharCreate.Male', MALE], ['Mods.SandboxWorld.CharCreate.Female', FEMALE]] as Array<[string, number[]]>) {
294	      const b = document.createElement('button');
295	      b.textContent = Lang.text(label);
296	      b.classList.toggle('active', pool.includes(this.appearance.skinVariant));
297	      b.addEventListener('click', () => {
298	        // 同性别的样式索引尽量保留（0↔4, 1↔5, 2↔6, 3↔7, 8↔9, 10↔11）
299	        const other = pool === MALE ? FEMALE : MALE;
300	        const idx = Math.max(0, other.indexOf(this.appearance.skinVariant));
301	        this.appearance.skinVariant = pool[idx] ?? pool[0];
302	        this.buildContent();
303	      });
304	      gRow.appendChild(b);
305	    }
306	    box.appendChild(gRow);
307	    this.content.appendChild(box);
308	  }
309	
310	  private buildLook() {
311	    const box = document.createElement('div');
312	    box.className = 'sw-char-styles';
313	    VALID_CLOTH_STYLES.forEach((styleId) => {
314	      const b = document.createElement('button');
315	      b.className = 'sw-char-stylebtn';
316	      b.title = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.CharCreate.StyleId', styleId);
317	      b.classList.toggle('active', this.appearance.skinVariant === styleId);
318	      const c = document.createElement('canvas');
319	      c.width = 40; c.height = 56;
320	      const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
321	      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
322	      const doll = compositePaperDoll({ ...this.appearance, skinVariant: styleId });
323	      if (doll) {
324	        const f = dollFrame(doll, IDLE_FRAME);
325	        ctx.drawImage(f.img, f.sx, f.sy, f.sw, f.sh, 0, 0, 40, 56);
326	      }
327	      b.appendChild(c);
328	      b.addEventListener('click', () => { this.appearance.skinVariant = styleId; this.buildContent(); });
329	      box.appendChild(b);
330	    });
331	    this.content.appendChild(box);
332	  }
333	
334	  private buildHair() {
335	    const wrap = document.createElement('div');
336	    wrap.className = 'sw-char-hairs';
337	    for (const hairId of CHAR_CREATION_HAIRS) {
338	      const b = document.createElement('button');
339	      b.className = 'sw-char-hairbtn';
340	      b.title = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.CharCreate.HairId', hairId);
341	      b.classList.toggle('active', this.appearance.hair === hairId);
342	      const c = document.createElement('canvas');
343	      c.width = 40; c.height = 44;
344	      const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
345	      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
346	      const doll = compositePaperDoll({ ...this.appearance, hair: hairId });
347	      if (doll) {
348	        // 头部区域（帧上部 44px，长发也能展示）
349	        ctx.drawImage(doll, 0, 0, 40, 44, 0, 0, 40, 44);
350	      }
351	      b.appendChild(c);
352	      b.addEventListener('click', () => { this.appearance.hair = hairId; this.buildContent(); });
353	      wrap.appendChild(b);
354	    }
355	    this.content.appendChild(wrap);
356	  }
357	
358	  private buildColor() {
359	    const box = document.createElement('div');
360	    box.className = 'sw-char-section';
361	    for (const row of COLOR_ROWS) {
362	      const el = document.createElement('label');
363	      el.className = 'sw-char-row';
364	      const span = document.createElement('span');
365	      span.textContent = Lang.text(row.labelKey);
366	      const key = row.key as keyof Appearance;
367	      const input = document.createElement('input');
368	      input.type = 'color';
369	      input.value = hex(this.appearance[key] as RGBColor);
370	      input.addEventListener('input', () => {
371	        (this.appearance[key] as RGBColor) = fromHex(input.value);
372	        
373	      });
374	      this.colorInputs.set(key, input);
375	      const rand = document.createElement('button');
376	      rand.textContent = '🎲';
377	      rand.title = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.CharCreate.RandomColor');
378	      rand.addEventListener('click', () => {
379	        const c = { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0 };
380	        const [r, g, b] = hslToRgb(Math.random(), 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.45, 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.35);
381	        c.r = Math.round(r * 255); c.g = Math.round(g * 255); c.b = Math.round(b * 255);
382	        (this.appearance[key] as RGBColor) = c;
383	        input.value = hex(c);
384	        
385	      });
386	      el.append(span, input, rand);
387	      box.appendChild(el);
388	    }
389	    this.content.appendChild(box);
390	  }
391	}
```

```
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.Personalities;

public class BiomePreferenceListTrait : IShopPersonalityTrait, IEnumerable<BiomePreferenceListTrait.BiomePreference>, IEnumerable
{
	public class BiomePreference
	{
		public AffectionLevel Affection;

		public AShoppingBiome Biome;

		public BiomePreference(AffectionLevel affection, AShoppingBiome biome)
		{
			Affection = affection;
			Biome = biome;
		}
	}

	private List<BiomePreference> _preferences;

	public BiomePreferenceListTrait()
	{
		_preferences = new List<BiomePreference>();
	}

	public void Add(BiomePreference preference)
	{
		_preferences.Add(preference);
	}

	public void Add(AffectionLevel level, AShoppingBiome biome)
	{
		_preferences.Add(new BiomePreference(level, biome));
	}

	public void ModifyShopPrice(HelperInfo info, ShopHelper shopHelperInstance)
	{
		BiomePreference biomePreference = null;
		for (int i = 0; i < _preferences.Count; i++)
		{
			BiomePreference biomePreference2 = _preferences[i];
			if (biomePreference2.Biome.IsInBiome(info.player) && (biomePreference == null || biomePreference.Affection < biomePreference2.Affection))
			{
				biomePreference = biomePreference2;
			}
		}
		if (biomePreference != null)
		{
			ApplyPreference(biomePreference, info, shopHelperInstance);
		}
	}

	private void ApplyPreference(BiomePreference preference, HelperInfo info, ShopHelper shopHelperInstance)
	{
		string nameKey = preference.Biome.NameKey;
		switch (preference.Affection)
		{
		case AffectionLevel.Love:
			shopHelperInstance.LoveBiome(nameKey);
			break;
		case AffectionLevel.Like:
			shopHelperInstance.LikeBiome(nameKey);
			break;
		case AffectionLevel.Dislike:
			shopHelperInstance.DislikeBiome(nameKey);
			break;
		case AffectionLevel.Hate:
			shopHelperInstance.HateBiome(nameKey);
			break;
		}
	}

	public IEnumerator<BiomePreference> GetEnumerator()
	{
		return _preferences.GetEnumerator();
	}

	IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()

```

````
AffectionLevel: Love=100 > Like=50 > Dislike=-50 > Hate=-100. ModifyShopPrice picks the preference with the HIGHEST affection among those whose biome matches (strictly greater replaces). So only ONE biome preference applies — the most positive.

Our current code applies like and dislike independently. That's a bug (double-counting when player is in a biome that's both... impossible actually — a player can be in only one shopping biome at a time? forest is the "default" fallback: our zones.forest is computed as "none of the others", so exactly one of the 8 zones is typically true... unless underground+something. E.g. Demolitionist likes underground, dislikes ocean. Can't be both. But snow+hallow? Guide likes forest dislikes ocean. Hmm — could a player be in both desert and underground (desert in cavern layer)? Yes! Then Merchant (like desert, dislike forest) — no. Dye Trader likes desert, dislikes forest: if in underground desert, zones.desert && zones.underground both true; his prefs are desert(like)/forest(dislike) — only desert matches → like. 

Consider Steampunker: like desert, dislike jungle — exclusive. Witch Doctor: like jungle, dislike hallow — exclusive. Mechanic: like snow, dislike underground — snow + underground can co-occur! Player in underground snow biome → zones.snow && zones.underground → vanilla applies ONLY like snow (higher affection). Our code applies both like (×0.94) and dislike (×1.06) = net 0.9964. BUG.

Also the preference LIST per NPC — our BIOME_PREFS table stores {like, dislike}. But vanilla PersonalityDatabasePopulator may register more than 2 (e.g. Santa has Love snow + Hate desert). Our table notes Santa has "like: 'snow'" mapped to LOVE_MUL special-cased. Order matters for ties — with distinct affections no tie issue.

So fix: apply only the highest-affection matching preference:
```ts
let best: {level, biomeKey} | null = null;
for (const p of prefs) if (zones[p.biomeKey] && (!best || affection(p.level) > affection(best.level))) best = p;
if (best) apply.
```
This changes price behavior in the snow+underground case — more faithful. The existing test at system-coverage.test.ts:131-137 tests single-biome cases, which still pass.
````

```
import { computeHappiness } from '../src/data/vanillaHappiness';

const baseHappy = {
  npcVanillaId: 17, homeless: false, distFromHome: 0,
  playerInCorrupt: false, playerInCrimson: false, playerInDungeon: false,
  zones: { forest: false, ocean: false, snow: false, desert: false, jungle: false, underground: false, hallow: false, mushroom: false },
  npcsInHouse: 0, npcsInVillage: 4, nearby: [], isPrincess: false, // village=4 压掉 LoveSpace
};

describe('NPC 快乐度/商店价格（ShopHelper）', () => {
  it('基准：森林 Merchant 有家无拥挤 → 1.0', () => {
    const r = computeHappiness({ ...baseHappy });
    expect(r.priceMul).toBe(1);
  });
  it('无家/离家>120/危险群系 → 1000（:111-122）', () => {
    expect(computeHappiness({ ...baseHappy, homeless: true }).priceMul).toBe(1000);
    expect(computeHappiness({ ...baseHappy, distFromHome: 121 }).priceMul).toBe(1000);
    expect(computeHappiness({ ...baseHappy, playerInCorrupt: true }).priceMul).toBe(1000);
  });
  it('拥挤：同屋>3 每人 ×1.05（5 人=×1.10 圆整）；宽敞 ×0.95（:126-157）', () => {
    expect(computeHappiness({ ...baseHappy, npcsInHouse: 5 }).priceMul).toBe(1.1);
    expect(computeHappiness({ ...baseHappy, npcsInHouse: 1, npcsInVillage: 2 }).priceMul).toBe(0.95); // LoveSpace
  });
  it('群系偏好：Merchant 喜欢 forest ×0.94 / dislike desert ×1.06；Santa Love/Hate ×0.88/×1.12', () => {
    expect(computeHappiness({ ...baseHappy, zones: { ...baseHappy.zones, forest: true } }).priceMul).toBe(0.94);
    expect(computeHappiness({ ...baseHappy, zones: { ...baseHappy.zones, forest: false, desert: true } }).priceMul).toBe(1.06);
    expect(computeHappiness({ ...baseHappy, npcVanillaId: 142, zones: { ...baseHappy.zones, forest: false, snow: true } }).priceMul).toBe(0.88);
    expect(computeHappiness({ ...baseHappy, npcVanillaId: 142, zones: { ...baseHappy.zones, desert: true } }).priceMul).toBe(1.12);
```

```
// 构建语言包:tools/l10n/(原版 12 语言 × 7 分片)+ tools/l10n-custom/(自有文案)
// → game/public/l10n/<culture>.json(扁平 { [category]: { [key]: value } })+ index.json
// 并生成 game/src/i18n/idNames.generated.ts(id → 原版常量名,id-maps internal 字段)。
//
// 合并顺序(键级,后者覆盖前者,等价于原版 ReloadLanguage 的"英文兜底→目标语言"语义,
// LanguageManager.cs:102):en 7 分片 → custom en → custom <target> → <target> 7 分片。
// {$Key} 复制构建期展开;含 {? 的键剔除(见 l10n-merge.mjs)。
//
// 用法:node scripts/build-l10n.mjs [--cultures=zh-Hans,en-US] [--skip-idnames]
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync, existsSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { parseLenient, flattenDeep, mergePacks, expandCopyCommands, stripConditionMarkers } from './l10n-merge.mjs';

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const TOOLS = join(here, '..', '..', 'tools');
const L10N = join(TOOLS, 'l10n');
const CUSTOM = join(TOOLS, 'l10n-custom');
const OUT_DIR = join(here, '..', 'public', 'l10n');
const IDMAPS = join(TOOLS, 'id-maps');
const IDNAMES_OUT = join(here, '..', 'src', 'i18n', 'idNames.generated.ts');

// GameCulture.cs:67-121(枚举值 = LegacyId);displayNameKey = 各语言包 Language 类别自带本地化名
export const CULTURES = [
  { name: 'en-US', legacyId: 1, displayNameKey: 'Language.English' },
  { name: 'de-DE', legacyId: 2, displayNameKey: 'Language.German' },
  { name: 'it-IT', legacyId: 3, displayNameKey: 'Language.Italian' },
  { name: 'fr-FR', legacyId: 4, displayNameKey: 'Language.French' },
  { name: 'es-ES', legacyId: 5, displayNameKey: 'Language.Spanish' },
  { name: 'ru-RU', legacyId: 6, displayNameKey: 'Language.Russian' },
  { name: 'zh-Hans', legacyId: 7, displayNameKey: 'Language.Chinese' },
  { name: 'pt-BR', legacyId: 8, displayNameKey: 'Language.Portuguese' },
  { name: 'pl-PL', legacyId: 9, displayNameKey: 'Language.Polish' },
  { name: 'ja-JP', legacyId: 10, displayNameKey: 'Language.Japanese' },
  { name: 'ko-KR', legacyId: 11, displayNameKey: 'Language.Korean' },
  { name: 'zh-Hant', legacyId: 12, displayNameKey: 'Language.ChineseTraditional' },
];

const SHARDS = ['', '.Game', '.Items', '.Legacy', '.NPCs', '.Projectiles', '.Town'];

const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const onlyArg = args.find((a) => a.startsWith('--cultures='));
const skipIdnames = args.includes('--skip-idnames');
const selected = onlyArg
  ? onlyArg.slice('--cultures='.length).split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)
  : CULTURES.map((c) => c.name);
const selectedCultures = CULTURES.filter((c) => selected.includes(c.name));

function readShards(culture) {
  return SHARDS.map((s) => {
    const f = join(L10N, `${culture}${s}.json`);
    if (!existsSync(f)) throw new Error(`缺分片: ${f}`);
    return parseLenient(readFileSync(f, 'utf8'));
  });
}

function readCustom(culture) {
  const f = join(CUSTOM, `${culture}.json`);
  if (!existsSync(f)) return null;
  return flattenDeep(parseLenient(readFileSync(f, 'utf8')));
}

mkdirSync(OUT_DIR, { recursive: true });

// en 基线(7 分片 + custom en)。同语言分片间的键冲突才是真告警,
// en→target 的"冲突"是翻译 overlay 的预期语义,不报告。
function mergeShards(culture) {
  const { out, conflicts } = mergePacks(readShards(culture));
  return { out, conflicts: conflicts.map((c) => `[${culture}] ${c}`) };
}
const enMerged = mergeShards('en-US');
const customEn = readCustom('en-US');
const enPacks = customEn ? [enMerged.out, customEn] : [enMerged.out];
const enBase = mergePacks(enPacks).out;

const report = { conflicts: enMerged.conflicts, unresolved: [], stripped: [] };
const index = [];

for (const cult of selectedCultures) {
  let table;
  if (cult.name === 'en-US') {
    table = enBase;
  } else {
    const targetMerged = mergeShards(cult.name);
    report.conflicts.push(...targetMerged.conflicts);
    const customTarget = readCustom(cult.name);
    const packs = customTarget ? [enBase, customTarget, targetMerged.out] : [enBase, targetMerged.out];
    table = mergePacks(packs).out;
  }

  const { unresolved } = expandCopyCommands(table);
  report.unresolved.push(...unresolved.map((u) => `[${cult.name}] ${u}`));
  report.stripped.push(...stripConditionMarkers(table).map((r) => `${cult.name}|${r}`));

  const text = JSON.stringify(table);
  writeFileSync(join(OUT_DIR, `${cult.name}.json`), text);
  const bytes = Buffer.byteLength(text, 'utf8');
  index.push({ name: cult.name, legacyId: cult.legacyId, displayNameKey: cult.displayNameKey, bytes });
  const cats = Object.keys(table).length;
  const keys = Object.values(table).reduce((n, e) => n + Object.keys(e).length, 0);

```

                                // Truffle（仅 Like 一档）\n};\ntype BiomeKey = keyof BiomeZones;\n\n/** 本仓 zone 键 → 原版 AShoppingBiome.NameKey（TownNPCMoodBiomes 分节键） */\nconst BIOME_NAME_KEY: 'Mushroom',\n};\n\nconst LIKE_MUL = 0.94, DISLIKE_MUL = 1.06, LOVE_MUL = 0.88, HATE_MUL = 1.12;\n/** AffectionLevel.cs:5-8（BiomePreferenceListTrait 最强命中比较用） */\nconst AFFECTION: Record<'Love' | 'Like' | 'Dislike' | 'Hate', number> =\n  { Love: 100, Like: 50, Dislike: -50, Hate: -100 };\n\n/** NPC 互相关系（AllPersonalitiesModifier.cs ModifyShopPrice_Relationships :12-506 全量 103 条；\n *  触发判定 = 目标在 owner 同屋列表（home 距离<25，GetNearbyResidentNPCs :295-332——村 25-120 不触发）；\n *  所有命中项连乘（与群系偏好的\"最强命中一条\"不同）。\n *  文案子键：Like/Love/Dislike/Hate 均为通用键（ShopHelper.LikeNPC/LoveNPC/DislikeNPC/HateNPC） */\ntype RelLevel = 'Love' | 'Like' | 'Dislike' | 'Hate';\nconst REL_KEY: Record<RelLevel, string> = { Love: 'LoveNPC', Like: 'LikeNPC', Dislike: 'DislikeNPC', Hate: 'HateNPC' };\nconst REL_MUL: Record<RelLevel, number> = { Love: LOVE_MUL, Like: LIKE_MUL, Dislike: DISLIKE_MUL, Hate: HATE_MUL };\nconst NPC_RELATIONSHIPS: Record<number, { target: number; level: RelLevel }[]> = {\n  17:  [{ target: 588, level: 38, level: 'Like' }, { target: 441, level: 'Like' }, { target: 550, level: 'Hate' }],\n  353: [{ target: 207, level: 'Love' }, { target: 229, level: 'Like' }, { target: 550, level: 'Dislike' }, { level: 'Like' }, { target: 160, level: 'Like' }, { target: 369, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 588, level: 'Hate' }],\n  550: [{ target: 38, level: 'Love' }, { target: 107, level: 'Like' }, { target: 107: [{ target: 124, level: 'Love' }, { target: 207, level: 'Like' }, { target: 54, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 353, level: 'Hate' }],\n  228: [{ target: 20, level: 'Like' }, { target: 22, level: 'Like' }, { target: 18, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 160, level: 'Hate' }],\n  54:  [{ target: 160, level: 'Love' }, { target: 441, level: 'Like' }, { target: 18, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 124, level: 'Hate' }],\n  124: [{ target: 107, level: 'Love' }, { target: 209, level: 'Like' }, { target: 19, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 54, level: 'Hate' }],\n  441: [{ target: 17, level: 'Love' }, { target: 208, level: 'Like' }, { target: 38, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 124, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 142, level: 'Hate' }],\n  229: [{ target: 369, level: 'Love' }, { target: 550, level: 'Like' }, { target: 353, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 22, level: 'Hate' }],\n  108: [{ target: 588, level: 'Love' }, { target: 17, level: 'Like' }, { target: 228, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 209, level: 'Hate' }],\n  178: [{ target: 209, level: 'Love' }, { target: 227, level: 'Like' }, { target: 208, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 108, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 20, level: 'Dislike' }],\n  209: [{ target: 353, level: 'Like' }, { target: 229, level: 'Like' }, { target: 178, level: 'Like' }, { target: 108, level: 'Hate' }, { target: 633, level: 'Dislike' }],\n  142: [{ target: 441, level: 'Hate' }],\n  588: [{ target: 227, level: 'Like' }, { target: 369, level: 'Love' }, { target: 17, level: 'Hate' }, { target: 229, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 633, level: 'Like' }],\n  22:  [{ target: 54, level: 'Like' }, { target: 178, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 227, level: 'Hate' }, { target: 633, level: 'Like' }],\n  160: [{ target: 22, level: 'Love' }, { target: 20, level: 'Like' }, { target: 54, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 228, level: 'Hate' }],\n  633: [{ target: 369, level: 'Dislike' }, { target: 19, level: 'Hate' }, { target: 228, level: 'Love' }, { target: 588, level: 'Like' }],\n};\n\nexport interface HappinessInput {\n  npcVanillaId: number;\n  /** NPC 无家（homeless） */\n  homeless: boolean;\n  /** NPC 当前中心 tile 距家 tile 距离 */\n  distFromHome: number;\n  /** 玩家（购物者）所在危险群系 */\n  playerInCorrupt: boolean; playerInCrimson: boolean; playerInDungeon: boolean;\n  /** 玩家群系 zone 快照（偏好判定用） */\n  zones: BiomeZones;\n  /** 同屋人数（<25 tile 内其它城镇 NPC，含不可同住门） */\n  npcsInHouse: number;\n  /** 村人数（25-120 tile） */\n  npcsInVillage: number;\n  /** 同屋目标 vanillaId 列表（<25，GetNearbyResidentNPCs 已过滤 37/368/453；\n   *  重复 id 折叠成位图语义——原版 nearbyNPCsByType 布尔） */\n  nearby: number[];\n  /** 本 NPC 是否公主（663） */\n  isPrincess: boolean;\n  /** 公主动态抽 Love 的随机源（:22-36 Main.rand.Next） */\n  rand?: () => number;\n}\n\nexport interface HappinessResult {\n  /** 价格乘子（LimitAndRoundMultiplier 后 ∈ [0.75, 1.5]；1.0 = 无修正） */\n  priceMul: number;\n  /** 心情报告行（原版 HappinessReport 逐条；空 = 无按钮，ReportHappiness.Condition） */\n  report: HappinessReportLine[];\n}\n\n/** 心情文案分节名（AddHappinessReportText :196） */\nexport function npcMoodCategory(npcVanillaId: number): string {\n  return `TownNPCMood_${NPC_NAME_BY_ID[npcVanillaId] ?? ''}`;\n}\n\n/** GetShoppingSettings / ProcessMood 1:1 */\nexport function computeHappiness(inp: HappinessInput): HappinessResult {\n  let mul = 1.0;\n  const report: HappinessReportLine[] = [];\n  const line = (sub: string, extra?: Partial<HappinessReportLine>) => {\n    report.push({ key: `${npcMoodCategory(inp.npcVanillaId)}.${sub}`, ...extra });\n  };\n  // remix/旅行商(368)/骷髅商(453)/城镇宠物/IsNotReallyTownNPC(老人37)——1.0 且无报告（:107-110）\n  if (inp.npcVanillaId === 368 || inp.npcVanillaId === 453 || inp.npcVanillaId === 37) {\n    return { priceMul: 1, report: [] };\n  }\n  // 无家 / 离家 >120 / 危险群系 → 置 1000（:111-122；末尾钳回 1.5）\n  if (inp.homeless) line('NoHome');                                  // RuinMoodIfHomeless :334-341\n  else if (inp.distFromHome > 120) line('FarFromHome');              // IsFarFromHome :343-353\n  for (const [flag, nameKey] of [\n    [inp.playerInCorrupt, 'Corruption'], [inp.playerInCrimson, 'Crimson'], [inp.playerInDungeon, 'Dungeon'],\n  ] as Array<[boolean, string]>) {\n    if (flag) { line('HateBiome', { biomeNameKey: nameKey }); break; }  // IsPlayerInEvilBiomes :355-370（首命中）\n  }\n  if (inp.homeless || inp.distFromHome > 120\n    || inp.playerInCorrupt || inp.playerInCrimson || inp.playerInDungeon) {\n    mul = 1000;\n  }\n  // 拥挤/宽敞（:123-157）。公主：拥挤系数 1.0（报告照发）且不吃 LoveSpace\n  const princess = inp.isPrincess;\n  const crowdPer = princess ? 1.0 : 1.05;\n  if (princess && inp.npcsInHouse < 2 && inp.npcsInVillage < 2) {\n    line('HateLonely');                                              // :132-137\n    mul = 1000;\n  }\n  if (inp.npcsInHouse > 3) {\n    for (let i = 3; i < inp.npcsInHouse; i++) mul *= crowdPer;       // :138-143\n    line(inp.npcsInHouse > 6 ? 'HateCrowded' : 'DislikeCrowded');\n  }\n  if (!princess && inp.npcsInHouse <= 2 && inp.npcsInVillage < 4) {\n    line('LoveSpace');                                               // :153-157\n    mul *= 0.95;\n  }\n  // 生物群系偏好（BiomePreferenceListTrait.ModifyShopPrice :40-55：命中中取好感最强一条）\n  const prefs = BIOME_PREFS[inp.npcVanillaId];\n  if (prefs) {\n    let best: { level: 'Love' | 'Like' | 'Dislike' | 'Hate'; biome: BiomeKey } | null = null;\n    for (const p of prefs) {\n      if (inp.zones[p.biome] && (!best || AFFECTION[p.level] > AFFECTION[best.level])) best = p;\n    }\n    if (best) {\n      line(`${best.level}Biome`, { biomeNameKey: BIOME_NAME_KEY[best.biome] });\n      mul *= REL_MUL[best.level] === LOVE_MUL ? LOVE_MUL\n        : best.level === 'Like' ? LIKE_MUL : best.level === 'Dislike' ? DISLIKE_MUL : HATE_MUL;\n    }\n  }\n  // NPC 互相关系（AllPersonalitiesModifier.ModifyShopPrice_Relationships :12-506——全部命中连乘）\n  const near = new Set(inp.nearby);\n  if (princess) {\n    // 公主无静态表：同屋池随机抽 3 个各 LoveNPCByTypeName ×0.88（:15-26；不足 3 全 Love）\n    const pool = [...near];\n    const rand = inp.rand ?? Math.random;\n    for (let j = 0; j < 3 && pool.length > 0; j++) {\n      const idx = Math.floor(rand() * pool.length) % pool.length;\n      const target = pool.splice(idx, 1)[0];\n      mul *= LOVE_MUL;\n      line(`LoveNPC_${NPC_NAME_BY_ID[target] ?? ''}`, { npcId: target });\n    }\n  } else {\n    if (near.has(663)) {                                             // :37-40\n      mul *= LIKE_MUL;\n      line('LikeNPC_Princess', { npcId: 663 });\n    }\n    for (const rel of NPC_RELATIONSHIPS[inp.npcVanillaId] ?? []) {\n      if (near.has(rel.target)) {\n        mul *= REL_MUL[rel.level];\n        line(REL_KEY[rel.level], { npcId: rel.target });\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  // 无报告 → Content；LimitAndRoundMultiplier（:175-186）\n  if (report.length === 0) line('Content');\n  mul = Math.max(0.75, Math.min(1.5, mul));\n  mul = Math.round(mul * 100) / 100;\n  return { priceMul: mul, report };\n}\n"
````
}
````

```json
}
```

```json
 "new_string": "  get isOpen(): boolean { return this.panel !== null; }\n}\n\n/** NPC 快乐度详情浮层（ReportHappiness.Interact：NPCInteractions.cs:303-313）。\n *  原版点「快乐度」按钮后把 HappinessReport 整段写进 npcChatText（Main.npcChatText =\n *  HappinessReport）+ 头像弹跳；DOM 对话框台词区定高，此处等价为独立浮层逐条列出，\n *  并附价格倍率（Main.cs:41240 ToString(\"P0\") 同口径）。 */\nexport class NpcHappinessPanel {\n  private panel: HTMLElement | null = null;\n  private escHandler: ((e: KeyboardEvent) => void) | null = null;\n\n  constructor(private parent: HTMLElement, private onClose: () => void) {}\n\n  open(info: HappinessInfo) {\n    this.close();\n    const face = happinessFace(info.priceMul);\n    const panel = document.createElement('div');\n    panel.className = 'sw-happy-panel';\n    const rows = (info.lines.length ? info.lines : ['—'])\n      .map((t) => `<div class=\"sw-happy-line\">${t}</div>`).join('');\n    panel.innerHTML = `\n      <div class=\"sw-happy-head\">\n        <div class=\"sw-happy-title\">${info.name}</div>\n        <span class=\"sw-happy-price ${face.cls}\">${face.face} ${happinessPct(info.priceMul)}</span>\n      </div>\n      <div class=\"sw-happy-list\">${rows}</div>\n      <div class=\"sw-happy-foot\"><button class=\"sw-happy-close\" style=\"background:#3a4680;color:#e8e8f4;border:1px solid #7d92d6;border-radius:4px;padding:5px 16px;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;\">${Lang.inter(52)}</button></div>`;\n    (panel.querySelector('.sw-happy-close') as HTMLElement).addEventListener('click', () => this.close());\n    this.parent.appendChild(panel);\n    this.panel = panel;\n    // 捕获阶段拦截 Esc：只关浮层，不连带关掉背后的对话框\n    this.escHandler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.code === 'Escape') { e.stopImmediatePropagation(); this.close(); }\n    };\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler, true);\n  }\n\n  close() {\n    if (this.escHandler) window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler, true);\n    this.escHandler = null;\n    this.panel?.remove();\n    this.panel = null;\n    this.onClose();\n  }\n\n  get isOpen(): boolean { return this.panel !== null; }\n}"
}
```
