<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
Wait: NpcHappinessPanel.close() calls this.onClose() even when called from within open() (this.close() at the start). The onClose callback in UI would set a field to null. Let me make the panel close silent for re-open: I'll guard — only call onClose when a panel was actually open. Simpler: in UI, onClose just clears the reference; calling it during open before re-setting is harmless if open() sets the reference AFTER. Actually in UI.showNpcHappiness I'd do:
```ts
this.npcHappiness = new NpcHappinessPanel(this.root, () => { this.npcHappiness = null; });
this.npcHappiness.open(info);
```
open() → close() → onClose() → this.npcHappiness = null (but we just assigned it, so it nulls the new instance's ref!). Bug. Fix: only invoke onClose if we had a panel:

```ts
close() {
  const had = this.panel !== null;
  ...
  if (had) this.onClose();
}
````

</details>

508	  onMusic?: (musicId: number) => void;

510	   *  由流程层回写 CharacterStore 持久化（原版 KillMeForGood :38173+ 直接删 .plr 档） */

513	
514	/** Main.time 口径（Main.cs 昼夜计时）：黎明 4:30（timeOfDay 0.25）归零，
515	 *  白天 54000 ticks、夜晚 32400 ticks。WorldGen.IsAlchemyPlantHarvestable（:66214）用。 */
516	function vanillaMainTime(timeOfDay: number): number {
517	  // timeOfDay↔Main.time：tick 恒速 86400t/周期 → 昼 (t-DAWN)*86400 / 夜 (t-DUSK mod 1)*86400
518	  if (timeOfDay >= Clock.DAWN && timeOfDay < Clock.DUSK) return (timeOfDay - Clock.DAWN) * 86400;
519	  return (((timeOfDay - Clock.DUSK) % 1) + 1) % 1 * 86400;

```

 "old_string": "  showNpcShop(title: string, items: Array<{ key: string; vanillaId: number; name: string; price: number }>, copper: number): void {\n    if (!this.npcShop) {\n      this.npcShop = new NpcShop(this.root,\n        (vid) => this.game?.npcShopBuy(vid),\n        () => { this.npcShop?.close(); this.npcShop = null; });\n    }\n    // 图标由 UI 侧按原版 id 补(atlas 管线)\n    const entries: ShopEntry[] = items.map((it) => ({\n      ...it,\n      iconUrl: this.game ? iconUrl(this.game, it.vanillaId) || '' : '',\n    }));\n    this.npcShop.open(title, entries, copper);\n  }",
 "new_string": "  showNpcShop(title: string, items: Array<{ key: string; vanillaId: number; name: string; price: number }>, copper: number, happinessPct?: number): void {\n    if (!this.npcShop) {\n      this.npcShop = new NpcShop(this.root,\n        (vid) => this.game?.npcShopBuy(vid),\n        () => { this.npcShop?.close(); this.npcShop = null; });\n    }\n    // 图标由 UI 侧按原版 id 补(atlas 管线)\n    const entries: ShopEntry[] = items.map((it) => ({\n      ...it,\n      iconUrl: this.game ? iconUrl(this.game, it.vanillaId) || '' : '',\n    }));\n    this.npcShop.open(title, entries, copper, happinessPct);\n  }\n\n  /** NPC 快乐度详情浮层（ReportHappiness.Interact） */\n  private npcHappiness: NpcHappinessPanel | null = null;\n\n  showNpcHappiness(info: HappinessInfo): void {\n    this.npcHappiness?.close();\n    this.npcHappiness = new NpcHappinessPanel(this.root, () => { this.npcHappiness = null; });\n    this.npcHappiness.open(info);\n  }\n\n  closeNpcHappiness(): void {\n    this.npcHappiness?.close();\n  }\n\n  /** 梳妆台更衣窗（Main.OpenClothesWindow Main.cs:43568）：复用角色创建面板的\n   *  外观/颜色页签，确认后回写角色档 */\n  private clothesPanel: CharCreation | null = null;\n\n  showClothesWindow(appearance: Appearance, onApply: (a: Appearance) => void): void {\n    this.clothesPanel = new CharCreation(this.root, {\n      // 更衣窗只改衣服样式+颜色：沿用 CharCreation 的克隆语义（确认回调拿到成品）\n      onCreate: (a) => onApply(a),\n      onCancel: () => { this.clothesPanel = null; },\n    }, { initial: appearance, dresser: true });\n  }"
}

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T06:00:38.893Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
Also: in dresser mode, `buildContent()` switch: `if (this.tab === 'info') ... else if hair ...` — with dresser tab = 'look' or 'color', fine. But if tab is 'info'/'hair' (can't happen in dresser). OK.

One more: the dresser panel's `close(this.cb.onCancel)` on Esc/back. UI.showClothesWindow sets clothesPanel but onCancel sets it to null — good. However, on apply (onCreate), we should also null clothesPanel. In UI I passed onCreate: (a) => onApply(a) — clothesPanel stays non-null (stale, panel removed). Let me fix:

```ts
showClothesWindow(appearance, onApply) {
  this.clothesPanel = new CharCreation(this.root, {
    onCreate: (a) => { this.clothesPanel = null; onApply(a); },
    onCancel: () => { this.clothesPanel = null; },
  }, { initial: appearance, dresser: true });
}
```
Note: CharCreation.close(fn) removes the panel then calls fn — so nulling is safe.

Also the mainFlow side: onClothesWindow(appearance) → open panel with onApply writing back to player.appearance + charStore + net resend.
````

</details>

                          treat 'git add -N' entries as real in the index
    -S <string>           look for differences that change the number of occurrences of the specified string
    -G <regex>            look for differences that change the number of occurrences of the specified regex
    --pickaxe-all         show all changes in the changeset with -S or -G
    --pickaxe-regex       treat <string> in -S as extended POSIX regular expression
    -O <file>             control the order in which files appear in the output
    --rotate-to <path>    show the change in the specified path first
    --skip-to <path>      skip the output to the specified path
    --find-object <object-id>
                          look for differences that change the number of occurrences of the specified object
    --diff-filter [(A|C|D|M|R|T|U|X|B)...[*]]
                          select files by diff type
    --output <file>       output to a specific file

'');\n    return s;\n  };\n  it('全部 25 个有偏好 NPC 的分节键存在于 zh-Hans 包（TownNPCMood_<Internal>）', () => {\n    for (const id of [22, 17, 588, 633, 441, 124, 209, 142, 207, 19, 178, 20, 228, 227,\n      369, 229, 353, 38, 107, 54, 108, 18, 208, 550, 160, 663]) {\n      expect(Lang.has(`TownNPCMood_${{ 22: 'Guide', 17: 'Merchant', 588: 'Golfer', 633: 'BestiaryGirl', 441: 'TaxCollector', 124: 'Mechanic', 209: 'Cyborg', 142: 'SantaClaus', 207: 'DyeTrader', 19: 'ArmsDealer', 178: 'Steampunker', 20: 'Dryad', 228: 'WitchDoctor', 227: 'Painter', 369: 'Angler', 229: 'Pirate', 353: 'Stylist', 38: 'Demolitionist', 107: 'GoblinTinkerer', 54: 'Clothier', 108: 'Wizard', 18: 'Nurse', 208: 'PartyGirl', 550: 'DD2Bartender', 160: 'Truffle', 663: 'Princess' }[id]}.Content`)).toBe(true);\n    }\n  });\n  it('报告行渲染后无残留占位符且非空（含替换）', () => {\n    const r = computeHappiness({ ...baseHappy, npcsInHouse: 1, nearby: [18], zones: { ...baseHappy.zones, forest: true } });\n    for (const line of r.report) {\n      const t = render(line);\n      expect(t.length, line.key).toBeGreaterThan(2);\n      expect(t, line.key).not.toContain('{');\n    }\n    // 快乐度按钮标签（UI.NPCCheckHappiness）\n    expect(Lang.text('UI.NPCCheckHappiness')).toBe('快乐度');\n  });\n  it('价格百分比与表情档位（Main.cs:41235-41243：≤0.82/≤1/≤1.1/其余 4 帧）', () => {\n    expect(happinessPct(1)).toBe('100%');\n    expect(happinessPct(0.75)).toBe('75%');\n    expect(happinessPct(1.5)).toBe('150%');\n    expect(happinessFace(0.75).cls).toBe('best');\n    expect(happinessFace(0.82).cls).toBe('best');\n    expect(happinessFace(0.94).cls).toBe('good');\n    expect(happinessFace(1.06).cls).toBe('ok');\n    expect(happinessFace(1.5).cls).toBe('bad');\n  });\n});"
